## Section 1 · Scope · what this ADR does and does not do

**This ADR (doctrine-only) DOES:**
- Restate the R-DOMAIN-01.v1.0.0 principles in enforceable form
- Define the LOGICAL distinction between LAM Object Type · Domain · Category as separate classification dimensions
- Inventory existing domain-like and category-like values across all known substrates and repo sources
- Reconcile those values (identify duplicates · aliases · conflicts · orphans · ambiguities)
- Propose a canonical status for each existing value with full source attribution
- Escalate any candidate value that requires founder authorisation to enter the canonical taxonomy

**This ADR does NOT:**
- Invent new taxonomy values
- Perform substrate writes
- Modify migrations or seed data
- Reclassify historical rows
- Lock a hierarchy between Domain and Category (R-DOMAIN-01 forbids that without a separate founder ADR)
- Author companion ADRs beyond this one
- Open Gate 3

**All escalations in §7 below are presented for founder decision · NOT resolved by Master AI autonomously.**

---

## Section 2 · Restated R-DOMAIN-01 principles (source of authority for this ADR)

Locked by Philip · 2026-09-11 · Candidate A · Three orthogonal classification dimensions:

> *"NEX maintains three orthogonal classification dimensions: LAM Object Type, Domain, and Category. LAM Object Type identifies the structural kind of NEX logical object; Domain identifies the subject-matter area the object concerns; Category identifies a governed classification within the relevant domain. No dimension may be silently substituted for another. Existing physical fields such as Supabase knowledge_records.category are physical representations of classification data and do not independently define NEX logical domain authority. Domain taxonomy, category taxonomy, and LAM Object Type taxonomy remain separately governed and versioned. A future hierarchical relationship between Domain and Category may only be introduced through an explicit founder-authorised ADR."*

Doctrinal principles enforced by ADR-0314a.2.s:

- **Three orthogonal dimensions.** LAM Object Type · Domain · Category. Any NEX logical object may carry up to three classification fields · one per dimension · appropriate to its object type.
- **No silent substitution.** A rule that says "per domain" MUST reference Domain · never LAM Object Type · never Category. Cross-dimension conflation is a G-2 non-bypass violation.
- **Physical fields ≠ logical authority.** Any existing physical field (Supabase `knowledge_records.category` · nex_dev `knowledge_feedback.domain` · etc.) is a physical representation. Its values may enter the canonical taxonomy only through this ADR's explicit reconciliation · not by inheritance from the field's existence.
- **Separate versioning.** LAM Object Type taxonomy version · Domain taxonomy version · Category taxonomy version — all separate.
- **No default hierarchy.** Domain and Category are NOT hierarchical unless a separate founder-authored ADR declares specific hierarchical relationships.

---

## Section 3 · Logical dimension definitions

### 3.1 · LAM Object Type · structural

**Authority:** ADR-0309.1 (Logical Authority Model)
**Governance:** 13 canonical types + Bridge from ADR-0312 = 14 total · versioned by ADR-0309.1

Canonical LAM Object Types (from ADR-0309.1 · illustrative here · not re-defined by this ADR):
Language · Semantic · People-Say · Domain Knowledge · Provenance · Governance · Truth Engine verdict · Contradiction · Confidence · Version · Runtime · Competency · Authorisation · Bridge.

**Logical meaning:** WHAT KIND of NEX logical object this row represents. Answers: "is this a language concept? a piece of domain knowledge? a truth-engine verdict? a bridge reference?"

**Not overlapping with Domain or Category** because it is orthogonal to subject-matter and to sub-classification within subject-matter.

### 3.2 · Domain · subject-matter scope

**Authority:** ADR-0314a.2 · R-DOMAIN-01.v1.0.0 (this ADR-0314a.2.s enumerates the taxonomy)
**Governance:** founder-authored · separately versioned as `domain_taxonomy.vN.N.N`

**Logical meaning:** WHICH SUBJECT-MATTER AREA the object concerns. Answers: "is this about staircases? kitchens? programming? accommodation? food?"

**Restrictions:**
- Domain is subject-matter · NOT structural (that's LAM Object Type)
- Domain is not sub-classification (that's Category)
- Domain values are governed slugs · not free-text
- Domain applies to Domain Knowledge, Semantic, People-Say, and (via bridge) most LAM object types · does NOT apply where the object is intrinsically domain-agnostic (e.g. a Confidence row · a Version row)

**Physical representations:** existing Supabase `knowledge_records.category` (with "NEX " prefix stripped) · existing nex_dev `knowledge_feedback.domain` field · existing `applicable_domain(s)` fields on external authority registry (deferred to ADR-0314a.2.p) · existing `subject_domain` on `nex-image-manifest.json` (per ADR-0024).

None of these physical fields DEFINE Domain. This ADR RECONCILES their values against the logical taxonomy.

### 3.3 · Category · governed classification within a domain

**Authority:** ADR-0314a.2 · R-DOMAIN-01.v1.0.0 (this ADR-0314a.2.s enumerates the taxonomy · per domain scope)
**Governance:** founder-authored per domain · separately versioned as `category_taxonomy.<domain>.vN.N.N` OR as a shared cross-domain category taxonomy if founder authorises

**Logical meaning:** GOVERNED SUB-CLASSIFICATION within the relevant Domain. Answers: "within the staircase domain, is this a tread specification? a handrail specification? a landing specification?" or "within the accommodation domain, is this a hotel? a hostel? a homestay?"

**Restrictions:**
- Category values are governed slugs · not free-text
- Category values are scoped to a Domain (or explicitly declared as cross-domain by founder)
- Category is NOT a Domain (no silent substitution)
- Category is NOT LAM Object Type

**Physical representations:** existing Supabase `knowledge_records.category` (currently mixes Domain and Category · reconciliation table in §6) · nex_dev `knowledge_records.subcategory` · nex `business_lead_directory.category_slug` · nex `business_lead_directory.categories[]` · marketplace taxonomy in `src/lib/nex-shop/taxonomy.ts` · accommodation category enum in `src/lib/nex/category-registry.ts`.

### 4.2 · Supabase substrate

**Finding:** NEX intelligence-substrate tables (`knowledge_records` · `knowledge_feedback` · related) do NOT live in Supabase migrations at this repo path. They live in `nex_dev` Postgres per §4.1. The Supabase migrations in this repo carry Hammerex tables and unrelated schemas.

**Hammerex tables (out of scope for NEX Intelligence · listed only for completeness):**
- `hammerex_xrated_downloads.category` · CHECK values: `brochure|form|compliance|catalogue|qualification|other` — Hammerex product · NOT NEX Intelligence
- `hammerex_xrated_faq_items.category` · CHECK values: `general|pricing|process|materials|trust|warranty|aftercare` — Hammerex FAQ · NOT NEX Intelligence
- `hammerex_xrated_news_posts.category` · CHECK values: `general` — Hammerex news · NOT NEX Intelligence

**Escalation note:** if founder wishes Supabase `knowledge_records` (which historical memory records as carrying values `"NEX door"` · `"NEX kitchen"` · `"NEX flooring"` · `"NEX staircase"` · `"NEX Materials"` · `"NEX Trade Knowledge · Design & Engineering"`) to be treated as an authoritative substrate for reconciliation, Master AI must be authorised to query Supabase read-only to enumerate the actual live values. This ADR presents the historical memory record only · without direct live inventory confirmation. See §7.1 escalation.

| `hostel` | category-registry | Accommodation domain | 🟢 CANONICAL CATEGORY candidate |
| `apartment` | category-registry | Accommodation domain | 🟢 CANONICAL CATEGORY candidate |
| `kos` | category-registry (countries: ["ID"] only) | Accommodation domain · Indonesia-specific | 🟡 CANONICAL CATEGORY candidate · locale-scoped · founder decides whether locale-scope is expressed via Category or a separate attribute |

**Note:** `accommodation` itself appears as a category-registry entry (broad) — this ADR proposes treating it as the Domain slug · not a Category within itself.

**Not proposed as Categories** (already handled elsewhere or out of scope):
- Accommodation intent slugs (~52) — these are QUESTION intents · not Category taxonomy
- Activity types (87) — see §7.4

### 6.4 · Category candidates · substrate-grounded · other domains

Business-classification categories (from `nex.business_lead_directory.category_group` if founder decides they are Categories within a Business/Commerce Domain rather than Domains themselves):

| slug | source | note |
|---|---|---|
| `retail`, `food-beverage`, `services`, `health`, `accommodation`, `other` | business_lead_directory + marketing_contact documented | §7.9 escalation · are these Domains or Categories? |

E-commerce categories (from `src/lib/nex-shop/taxonomy.ts` 12 master):
- Electronics · Fashion & Apparel · Home & Kitchen · Beauty & Personal Care · Health & Wellness · Sports & Outdoors · Toys & Hobbies · Automotive · Groceries & Gourmet Food · Pet Supplies · Office & Stationery · Tools & Home Improvement · Books Music & Movies
- §7.5 escalation · orphan against Domain taxonomy

Activity groups (from `src/lib/nex/intelligence-storage-grid/accommodation/activity-taxonomy.ts`):
- 11 groups (NATURE · OUTDOOR · CULTURE · ENTERTAINMENT · WELLNESS · FOOD_EXPERIENCE · SHOPPING · NIGHTLIFE · TRANSPORT_HUB · SERVICE · OTHER) · 87 activity types
- §7.4 escalation · Category taxonomy WITHIN what Domain?

---

## Section 7 · Escalations · founder decisions required before canonical taxonomy versions can lock

Each escalation below identifies a value or a structural question that requires founder authorisation. Master AI does NOT resolve these autonomously.

### §7.1 · Direct live substrate confirmation

**Question:** May Master AI perform read-only Supabase queries to enumerate the actual current values in `knowledge_records.category` (and any similar fields on Supabase-side tables) so that reconciliation uses live values rather than historical memory record?

**Why:** MEMORY.md records example values `"NEX door"` · `"NEX kitchen"` · etc. as historical observations. Doctrine 2026-09-09 states "Memory informs context. Memory does not establish truth." A live read-only query would give substrate-verified inventory · not memory-derived.

**Impact if not authorised:** the reconciliation table in §5 must treat Supabase historical values as UNVERIFIED · marked accordingly in the canonical proposal.

**Founder decision required.**

### §7.2 · `food` vs `food-beverage`

**Question:** Are `food` and `food-beverage` (a) synonyms of the same canonical Domain (with one being the canonical slug and the other an alias) OR (b) distinct Domains?

**Existing sources:**
- `food`: `nex_lab.rooms` seed · `NexVertical` enum
- `food-beverage`: `nex.business_lead_directory.category_group` documented · `nex.marketing_contact.category_group` documented

**Proposal for founder:**
- (a) `food` becomes canonical · `food-beverage` becomes documented alias → simplest
- (b) `food` is subject-matter Domain · `food-beverage` is a business-classification Category within `business` Domain → separation of subject-matter from commercial-classification

**Founder decision required.**

### §7.3 · `nex_lab.rooms` seed values that are NOT subject-matter

**Question:** Do the following `nex_lab.rooms` seed values become Domain slugs, or are they operational-scope labels that live OUTSIDE the Domain taxonomy?

- `image` · `news` · `voice` · `chat` · `monetization`
- Also `master_ai` from agent-runtime · which is operational not subject-matter

**Concern:** Domain per R-DOMAIN-01 is "subject-matter area the object concerns." A row about staircases has Domain=staircase · but a row about NEX's speaking capability is not obviously about a subject-matter "voice" — it's an operational scope.

**Proposal for founder:**
- (a) exclude these from Domain taxonomy · they remain nex_lab operational room slugs but do NOT become Domain values
- (b) include as Domains anyway · treat "voice" as subject-matter (speech/audio content)
- (c) create a separate axis for operational scopes distinct from Domain

**Founder decision required.**

Also affected: `marketing` (ADR-0033 brain) and `activities` (nex_lab room · plural) — same operational-vs-subject-matter question.

### §7.4 · Activity taxonomy · where does it belong?

**Question:** The 11 activity groups + 87 activity types in `src/lib/nex/intelligence-storage-grid/accommodation/activity-taxonomy.ts` were authored inside the accommodation module. But conceptually many values (airport · atm · pharmacy · hospital · etc.) are cross-domain.

**Proposal for founder:**
- (a) treat activity-taxonomy as Category-WITHIN-Accommodation taxonomy only · other Domains re-author their own activity-like categories independently
- (b) hoist to a shared Category taxonomy with `applicable_domains` = ["accommodation", "food", "transport", "travel"]
- (c) split · e.g. TRANSPORT_HUB group moves to a Transport Domain · SERVICE group becomes cross-domain · etc.

**Founder decision required.**

### §7.5 · Marketplace taxonomy orphan

**Question:** The 12 master e-commerce categories in `src/lib/nex-shop/taxonomy.ts` (Electronics · Fashion & Apparel · etc.) have no obvious Domain parent in the reconciled taxonomy.

**Proposal for founder:**
- (a) create a Domain slug (candidate: `commerce` OR `retail` OR `marketplace`) that parents the e-commerce master categories · this is a genuinely-new Domain value · requires founder decision
- (b) treat marketplace taxonomy as a separate physical-representation not part of the NEX Domain/Category logical taxonomy (Marketplace is a nex-shop concern · not NEX Intelligence)
- (c) map each e-commerce master to an existing Domain candidate (Electronics → `tools`? · Home & Kitchen → `kitchen`? · Tools & Home Improvement → `tools`? · etc.) · risky · likely wrong

**Founder decision required · this is the largest orphan set (12+ values).**

### §7.6 · Alias resolution questions

Multiple slug-pairs need founder decision on whether they are aliases · synonyms · or distinct Domains:

- `engineering` (agent-runtime) vs `programming` (implied by code-intent-registry)
- `voice` (nex_lab.rooms) vs `speaking` (agent-runtime)
- `transport` (nex_lab.rooms) vs `travel` (agent-runtime)
- `rentals` (NexVertical) — relation to `accommodation`? (short-term rentals are accommodation; equipment rentals are not)

**Founder decision required for each.**

### §7.7 · `other` sentinel

**Question:** Does `other` become a canonical Domain sentinel value OR does the taxonomy have NO sentinel (fail-closed to `unknown_domain` if no match)?

**Concern:** ADR-0314 Rule TE1 · "unknown is a verified fact not a fabrication." An `other` sentinel invites soft-fallback classifications. A fail-closed policy avoids that but requires every classifiable object to have a real Domain match.

**Founder decision required.**

### §7.8 · Supabase `"NEX "` prefix

**Question:** The historical Supabase `knowledge_records.category` values (memory records: `"NEX door"` · `"NEX kitchen"` · `"NEX flooring"` · `"NEX staircase"` · `"NEX Materials"` · `"NEX Trade Knowledge · Design & Engineering"`) carry a `"NEX "` presentation prefix.

**Proposal for founder:**
- (a) canonical slug strips the prefix (`door` · `kitchen` · etc.) and the mapping ADR (this one or a companion) records the physical-representation transform
- (b) canonical slug preserves the prefix
- (c) treat Supabase category values as Category (not Domain) values within a broader Domain (e.g. `trades` or `construction`) · reclassify at reconciliation time · requires founder direction

**Founder decision required · this affects potentially thousands of Supabase rows.**

### §7.9 · Business-classification tier: Domain or Category?

**Question:** Are the 6 business_lead_directory + marketing_contact documented values (`retail` · `food-beverage` · `services` · `health` · `accommodation` · `other`) (a) Domains OR (b) Categories within a Business/Commerce Domain?

**Proposal for founder:**
- (a) each is a Domain in its own right · consistent with agent-runtime and NexVertical framing
- (b) each is a Category within a `business` Domain OR `commerce` Domain · consistent with §7.5 marketplace escalation

**Founder decision required.**

### §7.10 · Genuinely-new Domain values needed to complete reconciliation

If founder wishes reconciliation to be COMPLETE across all existing sources, some slugs likely need to be created that do not yet exist in any substrate:

- `programming` (or `code`) — for code-intent-registry parent Domain
- `commerce` (or similar) — for marketplace-taxonomy parent Domain (see §7.5)
- Possibly `trades` OR `construction` (or similar) — for reconciling ADR-0033 brains list which currently sits without a Domain parent · could be the parent Domain of `staircase` · `door` · `kitchen` · `bathroom` · `flooring` · `roofing` · `lighting` · `tools` · `timber` · `interior`

**These are genuinely-new values. Master AI does NOT create them. Founder decides each individually.**

**Alternative:** founder authors the taxonomy with FEWER Domains (e.g. `staircase` · `kitchen` · `door` etc. are top-level Domains · no `trades` parent) which is exactly what R-DOMAIN-01 permits (no default hierarchy).

**Founder decision required.**

---

## Section 8 · Proposed taxonomy governance (draft · founder confirms)

### 8.1 · Versioning

- **Domain taxonomy version:** `domain_taxonomy.v0.0.1-draft` while this ADR is under review · promotes to `v1.0.0` when founder locks the canonical set
- **Category taxonomy version:** per-Domain OR shared · founder decides · versioned similarly
- Every future addition/removal of a canonical Domain or Category value requires a new taxonomy version and a new founder-authorised ADR

### 8.2 · Deprecation policy

- Deprecated Domain values remain in the taxonomy registry with `status=deprecated` for at least one major taxonomy version
- Physical fields carrying deprecated values are not silently rewritten; explicit migration ADR required
- Guardian rejects writes with deprecated values unless the write is a migration-authorised backfill

### 8.3 · Physical-representation mapping registry

The final ADR-0314a.2.s (post-founder-review) SHOULD include a mapping table:

| physical field | canonical Domain slug | canonical Category slug (if any) | transform |
|---|---|---|---|
| Supabase `knowledge_records.category` = `"NEX door"` | `door` | (empty or the row's specific Category) | strip `"NEX "` prefix |
| nex_dev `knowledge_feedback.domain` = `staircase` | `staircase` | — | identity |
| `nex_lab.rooms.room_slug` = `accommodation` | `accommodation` | — | identity |
| ... | ... | ... | ... |

Draft only · pending §7.1 · §7.8 · §7.3 resolution.

### 8.4 · Anti-substitution enforcement

Guardian rejects writes where:
- A field declared as `domain` receives a value from the Category taxonomy
- A field declared as `category` receives a value from the Domain taxonomy
- A field declared as `lam_object_type` receives a value from Domain or Category taxonomy
- A field receives a value not present in the currently-locked taxonomy version

Rejection reason string: `r_domain_01_anti_substitution_or_unregistered_value`.

### 8.5 · Cross-referencing with existing ADRs

- **ADR-0033** (brains isolation) — brain slugs consumed by NEX classifier for image routing MUST match canonical Domain slugs after this ADR locks · silent divergence rejected
- **ADR-0310 · R4** — physical-storage-location doesn't-determine-authority · reinforced by this ADR (Supabase category is a physical representation · not authoritative)
- **ADR-0312** — Bridge references may carry `domain` field; must use canonical Domain slug
- **ADR-0314a.2** — 12 locked founder-resolutions that reference "domain" (R-01 · R-05 · R-07 · R-13 · indirectly R-02) all resolve their references to the canonical Domain slugs authored here

---

## Section 9 · What this ADR did NOT do

- ❌ No canonical Domain taxonomy locked · candidates only · founder decides
- ❌ No canonical Category taxonomy locked · candidates only · founder decides
- ❌ No physical-representation transform locked · draft mapping proposed
- ❌ No values invented · every value traced to at least one existing substrate or repo source
- ❌ No substrate touched · no migration · no seed · no CHECK constraint added or modified
- ❌ No historical row modified
- ❌ Gate 3 not opened
- ❌ ADR-0314a.2 twelve locked founder-resolutions untouched (all remain v1.0.0)
- ❌ R-DOMAIN-01.v1.0.0 untouched (remains as founder authored)

## Section 11 · §7.1 · Founder authorisation for read-only Supabase query · 2026-09-11

**Founder authorisation verbatim:**

> *"§7.1: YES — Master AI is authorised to perform read-only queries against the live Supabase NEX intelligence substrate solely to verify the actual current knowledge_records.category values."*
>
> *"The query must be strictly read-only and must not modify, normalize, migrate, classify, promote, deprecate or otherwise alter any data."*
>
> *"Preserve the exact observed values and report:*
> - *raw value*
> - *row count*
> - *physical substrate*
> - *source/table/field*
> - *existing status distribution where available*
> - *whether the value has an existing logical interpretation*
> - *whether it appears to be an alias, duplicate, ambiguous value or genuine distinct value"*
>
> *"Clearly separate observed live taxonomy values from proposed canonical taxonomy values. Do not invent, rename, merge, delete or canonicalize any value."*
>
> *"After §7.1 evidence is collected, STOP and present §7.2 only. No other founder decision may be assumed."*
>
> — Philip · 2026-09-11

**Locked constraints for the §7.1 query:**

- SELECT / read only · absolutely no INSERT · UPDATE · DELETE · ALTER · DDL · function invocation
- No status changes on any row · no promotion · no demotion · no deprecation
- No normalisation of values (preserve `"NEX door"` as `"NEX door"` · never fold to `"door"`)
- No merging of duplicates · no deletion of orphans · no renaming
- No canonicalisation · no proposed-mapping applied at query time
- No invention of derived taxonomy values
- Query scope: `knowledge_records.category` (primary) · plus adjacent fields on the same substrate that carry classification data (`subcategory` if it exists in the live schema · `domain` if it exists) — read-only inventory only
- Any adjacent-field additions must be reported as such · not silently expanded scope

**Report structure (locked before query execution):**

Per observed distinct value, one row in the inventory:

| raw_value | row_count | physical_substrate | source_table.field | status_distribution | existing_logical_interpretation | alias/duplicate/ambiguous/distinct flag |

Plus a header block:
- total rows scanned
- total distinct values found
- SQL used (verbatim)
- Timestamp of query
- Substrate connection method (which client · which credentials scope · which project)

**What this query does NOT do:**
- Does not propose canonical taxonomy values
- Does not resolve §7.2–§7.10
- Does not update ADR-0314a.2.s canonical inventory
- Does not touch any nex_dev substrate
- Does not touch any repo hardcoded taxonomy
- Does not alter Supabase state in any way

**After query completes:** append the inventory report as **Section 12 · §7.1 evidence** to this ADR · then STOP. Founder presents §7.2 next.

### 12.3 · Structural observations · NO CANONICAL DECISIONS · founder decides

The Supabase `knowledge_records.category` field appears to carry values from **three overlapping usage patterns**. This is an observation about the substrate · NOT a reclassification proposal.

**Usage pattern A · Bare subject-matter labels · `"NEX " + <lowercase-slug>`:**
- Values: `"NEX door"` (2088) · `"NEX flooring"` (620) · `"NEX kitchen"` (458) · `"NEX staircase"` (49) — **3215 rows / 88.7% of substrate**
- All carry `subcategory = "auto-extracted (mock adapter)"`
- All carry `authorised_by = NULL`
- Status heavily DRAFT (86% of the 3215) with some DEPRECATED / UNDER_REVIEW
- 100% `primary_audience = homeowner`
- Interpretation deferred to founder · these superficially look like Domain-shaped values

**Usage pattern B · Hierarchical dotted paths · `"NEX " + <Word Case> [+ " · " + <Word Case>]*`:**
- Values with " · " separator: 66 / 86 (77% of distinct values · but only ~10% of rows)
- Example depths: 1-level (`"NEX Trade Knowledge"`) · 2-level (`"NEX Trade Knowledge · Design & Engineering"`) · 3-level (`"NEX Trade Knowledge · Design & Engineering · Geometries"`) · 4-level (`"NEX Trade Knowledge · Materials · Hardwood · North American"`)
- Status heavily AUTHORITATIVE with some UNDER_REVIEW/DEPRECATED
- Audience mostly manufacturer with some engineer/homeowner
- Interpretation deferred to founder · these superficially look like hierarchical Category paths · but note R-DOMAIN-01 explicitly forbids default hierarchy · founder decides

**Usage pattern C · Operational / structural labels:**
- Values that appear to describe operational scopes rather than subject-matter: `"NEX AI Rules"` (1) · `"NEX Concepts"` (1) · `"NEX Business Operating System"` (1) · `"NEX Business Operating System · Platform Knowledge"` (2) · `"NEX Platform Architecture · Knowledge Management"` (6) · `"NEX Trade Platform"` (1) · `"NEX Technical Documentation"` (6) · `"NEX Architecture"` (2) — **~15 rows across 8 values**
- Interpretation deferred to founder · these look like operational scopes (per feedback memory 2026-09-11 · founder principle "don't turn every 'domain' word into a NEX Domain")

### 12.4 · Observed pairs · candidate aliases / candidate duplicates / candidate ambiguities · founder decides

The following pairs OR clusters exhibit textual similarity that could indicate alias / duplicate / near-synonym relationships. Master AI reports the observation · does NOT resolve.

### 13.2 · Exact semantic meaning in each source

- **`food` in `nex_lab.rooms`** — a Lab operational room · seeded with data sources OSM + kemenparekraf · target 250,000 records · display "Food Lab" · a subject-matter substrate slice for collecting food-related knowledge from public sources.
- **`food` in `NexVertical` enum + `CATEGORY_REGISTRY`** — a canonical top-level vertical · one of three (food · accommodation · rentals) · parent of forthcoming food sub-categories · owns route `/food` · owns table `nex.food_business` · brainKeywords include `"food", "makanan", "restaurant", "restoran", "eat", "makan"`. Constitutional per Philip 2026-08-22 category-wheel amendment: *"There must be one canonical category identity."*
- **`food` in `domain-classifier`** — the Domain label that user tokens `food` and `restaurant` both resolve to (i.e. `restaurant` is subsumed under Domain `food`).
- **`food` in `worker-food` / `food-postgres` / `food-adapter`** — the AgentId + Domain identifier used across worker registration · brain adapter · and live-chat adapter · a coherent subject-matter Domain in code.
- **`food-beverage` in `business_lead_directory.category_group`** — a business-classification tier value · one of six categories for classifying businesses that appear in the broad business_lead_directory · used to route rows to specialist verticals.
- **`food-beverage` in `marketing_contact.category_group`** — mirror of the business_lead_directory taxonomy · used for outbound marketing segmentation.
- **`food-beverage` in `business-lead-executor.ts:43`** — the value that TRIGGERS routing away from the broad directory INTO the food specialist vertical. Direct code evidence that food-beverage-category rows are treated as members of the food Domain.
- **`food-beverage/{fast-food-chain, warung-local, coffee-cafe, restaurant, bakery, bar-nightlife}` in Lab logs** — 6 compound sub-slugs under food-beverage · granular business-type classifications of food-related businesses.
- **`food_beverage` in `nex.mp_category` seed** — a marketplace product category · one of 19 marketplace categories · sort_order 130 · display "Food & Beverage". A marketplace-vertical scope, orthogonal to Domain in the R-DOMAIN-01 sense (marketplace product categorisation).
- **`food_beverage` in `nex-market/page.tsx`** — UI icon mapping for the marketplace category (🥐).
- **`FOOD_EXPERIENCE` in `activity-taxonomy.ts`** — an accommodation-scoped Category group · 3 activities near accommodation businesses · orthogonal to Domain (accommodation Category · food adjacency).
- **`nearby_food` in accommodation `intent-registry`** — an accommodation-scoped intent · not itself a Domain value · uses food/restaurant/cafe tokens.

### 13.3 · Alias / synonym evidence

The strongest piece of alias evidence is code-level:

> **`src/lib/nex/lab/executors/business-lead-executor.ts:6-8` and `:43`** — the routing rule that says *"if `category_group === 'food-beverage'` then this row does NOT go to `nex.business_lead_directory` · it goes to `nex.food_business`"*. This means every `food-beverage`-tagged row is routed to the food specialist vertical whose Domain identifier is `food`. The code treats them as the same subject.

Secondary alias evidence:

- `domain-classifier` maps user token `restaurant` (a food-beverage business type) to Domain `food` · the same Domain classifier used elsewhere.
- Lab categoriser logs show `food-beverage/*` compound slugs, all of which describe food-service businesses — no evidence of `food-beverage` being used to classify anything other than food-and-drink establishments.
- `nex.food_business` is the specialist table · there is NO separate `nex.beverage_business` or `nex.food_and_beverage_business` table in the substrate.
- Category-registry brainKeywords under `id: "food"` include `restaurant` and `restoran` (Indonesian for restaurant) · Bahasa Indonesia term `makan` (eat) · demonstrating that food covers eat/drink/restaurant subject-matter as a unified concept.

Contrary evidence (arguing for distinct Domains):

- `food_beverage` (underscore variant) appears as a marketplace product Category · not a NEX Intelligence Domain reference. But this is a different logical layer (marketplace vs intelligence) and does not argue for distinct Domain within Intelligence.
- `FOOD_EXPERIENCE` in accommodation activity-taxonomy separately enumerates food-nearby-accommodation as a category · but this is a Category within Accommodation Domain, orthogonal to a Food Domain.
- **No substrate anywhere has a `beverage`-only value.** No `nex_lab.rooms` slug `beverage` · no NexVertical `beverage` · no domain-classifier mapping `beverage` · no worker-beverage · no beverage-adapter. If beverage were a distinct Domain, we would expect at least one artefact.

### 13.4 · Same-subject-domain vs genuinely-distinct-domain analysis

**Same-subject-domain interpretation:**

- `food` is the canonical Domain slug used consistently across 6+ NEX subsystems (Lab · Vertical · Classifier · Worker · Brain adapter · Chat adapter · Business table).
- `food-beverage` is a business-classification tier label used within the business-directory / marketing-contact / lab-categoriser layer to group businesses that produce/serve food and drink.
- The routing evidence in `business-lead-executor.ts` DIRECTLY establishes that food-beverage-tagged rows belong to the food specialist vertical · not a separate vertical.
- Every `food-beverage/<sub>` compound slug describes a food-service business type · no exception.

**Genuinely-distinct-domain interpretation:**

- Would require a substrate artefact that treats `food-beverage` as a peer to `food` at the Domain layer · NONE observed.
- Would require an operational reason to separate the two · NONE observed in the current codebase.

**Conclusion of analysis:** the evidence weight is heavily in favour of same-subject-domain. `food-beverage` and `food_beverage` (underscore) function as either aliases/synonyms of `food` when used in a Domain-shaped position, OR as Category-tier labels when used within the business-classification layer.

### 13.5 · Consequences of each possible decision

**Consequence Table A · food and food-beverage are the same Domain (food canonical · food-beverage alias):**

| Consequence | Impact |
|---|---|
| Doctrine · food is the canonical Domain slug | Aligns with 6+ existing subsystems that already use `food` |
| Doctrine · food-beverage is a documented alias when used in a Domain-shaped position | Preserves existing business_lead_directory / marketing_contact terminology without renaming any live row |
| Doctrine · food-beverage-tagged rows in business_lead_directory route to the food vertical | Aligns with existing `business-lead-executor.ts:43` code · zero code change required |
| Doctrine · food-beverage-tier compound slugs (e.g. food-beverage/fast-food-chain) may become Category values within the food Domain in the future | Deferred to Category taxonomy authoring (later companion ADR) · no lock now |
| Doctrine · underscore variant `food_beverage` (marketplace) is orthogonal to Intelligence Domain taxonomy | Marketplace category taxonomy remains separate from NEX Intelligence Domain (aligns with §7.5 orphan escalation) |
| Physical field rewrites required | **NONE** · alias declaration does not rename any live value |
| Migrations required | **NONE** · doctrine-only |
| Guardian impact | Guardian recognises `food-beverage` and `food_beverage` as documented aliases of Domain `food` when reading Domain fields · rejects them if used at LAM-Object-Type field |
| Fragmentation risk | LOW · one Domain owns the subject-matter · aliases documented rather than distinct |
| Reversibility | HIGH · a future founder ADR may promote food-beverage to a distinct Domain if a business need arises · alias declaration does not lock out that path |

**Consequence Table B · food and food-beverage are distinct Domains:**

| Consequence | Impact |
|---|---|
| Doctrine · food is a Domain · food-beverage is a separate Domain | Creates fragmentation across 6+ subsystems that treat them as one subject |
| Doctrine · food-beverage-tagged rows can no longer implicitly route to the food vertical | Requires reviewing `business-lead-executor.ts:43` and either amending it OR authoring a distinct beverage-business specialist |
| Physical substrate impact | Nothing renamed but the DIVERGENCE between routing code (treats food-beverage → food) and doctrine (treats them as distinct) becomes a violation |
| Migrations required | Potentially: new specialist beverage-business table · or amendment to food-business to explicitly cover beverage · or a bridge table |
| Guardian impact | Guardian must recognise TWO Domain values and reject any silent conflation |
| Fragmentation risk | HIGH · knowledge splits without clear subject boundary · specialist workers · adapters · Q&A intents all fragment |
| Reversibility | LOW · once distinct Domains ship · merging them later requires substrate rewrites |
| Substantive gain | NONE observable in the current NEX use cases · beverage-only businesses appear naturally covered by `food-beverage/bar-nightlife` |

**Consequence Table C · food and food-beverage are BOTH Categories within a broader parent Domain (e.g. "consumables" or "hospitality"):**

| Consequence | Impact |
|---|---|
| Requires introducing a new parent Domain value | Genuinely-new value · triggers §7.10 escalation · not answerable at §7.2 scope |
| Doctrine · food and food-beverage become sibling Categories under a common parent | Complete restructure of existing food subsystems (Lab · Vertical · Classifier · Worker) which are all keyed on Domain `food` |
| Physical substrate impact | Large · potentially rename `nex.food_business` and cascade |
| Fragmentation risk | Very high |
| Substantive gain | NONE observable · no NEX use case demands a parent-Domain currently |
| Deferrable? | Yes · founder may address in §7.9 (business-classification tier: Domains or Categories within Business/Commerce Domain?) |

### 13.6 · Recommendation (LABELLED · founder decides)

**Recommendation only (not binding · not doctrine · founder authorises the decision text):**

Master AI recommends **Consequence Table A** (`food` canonical Domain · `food-beverage` alias). Reasons:

1. Every existing NEX subsystem (Lab · Vertical · Classifier · Worker · Brain adapter · Chat adapter · Business table · UI) already treats food as the canonical Domain and food-beverage as a business-classification tier label under it.
2. The routing code (`business-lead-executor.ts:43`) has already made the operational decision that food-beverage-tagged rows belong to the food vertical. Formalising this as alias-doctrine ratifies existing behaviour rather than changing it.
3. `food-beverage/{6 sub-slugs}` in the Lab categoriser is naturally readable as *"food-Category-tier within food-Domain"* if food-beverage becomes a Category tier · or as *"alias of food when used at Domain layer"* if food-beverage is treated as Domain-shaped. Both remain compatible with Consequence Table A · neither is precluded.
4. Consequence Table B introduces fragmentation with no observable use case.
5. Consequence Table C requires a genuinely-new Domain value that is out of §7.2 scope (§7.9/§7.10 territory).
6. Reversibility is HIGH · a future founder-authorised ADR can promote food-beverage to a distinct Domain if the business case emerges.

**Master AI does not lock this decision. Founder authorises via the wording in §13.7 or amends.**

### 13.7 · Founder decision · A1 · 🔒 LOCKED 2026-09-11

Two candidate wordings presented · founder chooses OR amends OR writes their own:

**Candidate wording A1 (concise · aligned with founder's pre-signalled direction):**

> *"§7.2 · `food` is the canonical NEX Domain slug for the subject-matter of food and beverage. `food-beverage` (and its physical-substrate underscore variant `food_beverage`) is treated as an alias/synonym of the `food` Domain when the value appears in a Domain-shaped position. When `food-beverage` appears at the business-classification tier (e.g. as `nex.business_lead_directory.category_group` values with compound sub-slugs such as `food-beverage/fast-food-chain`), the outer `food-beverage` label is a Category-tier label within the `food` Domain and does not create a distinct Domain. Marketplace `food_beverage` in `nex.mp_category` is a marketplace product category orthogonal to the NEX Intelligence Domain taxonomy · its status is settled by §7.5. A future founder-authorised ADR may promote `food-beverage` to a genuinely distinct Domain if a business boundary arises · this ADR does not do so. R-DOMAIN-01 anti-substitution rules apply · Guardian rejects use of `food-beverage` at LAM Object Type or Category layers when the intent is Domain-shaped."*

**Candidate wording A2 (minimal · defers alias-tier detail to Category authoring):**

> *"§7.2 · `food` is the canonical NEX Domain slug for the subject-matter of food and beverage. `food-beverage` is not a distinct Domain. Existing substrate uses of `food-beverage` remain unchanged; their canonical Domain interpretation is `food` unless a later founder-authorised ADR establishes a distinct subject boundary."*

**Master AI's recommendation between A1 and A2 (labelled · not binding at time of proposal):**

Recommend **A1** because it explicitly addresses the underscore variant · the marketplace overlap · the Category-tier interpretation · and the anti-substitution rule · reducing later ambiguity. A2 is acceptable if founder prefers minimum doctrine surface.

**Load-bearing architectural basis:** the routing code `src/lib/nex/lab/executors/business-lead-executor.ts:43` explicitly folds `food-beverage`-classified rows into `nex.food_business` (the food specialist substrate). Every food-related substrate in NEX (NexVertical enum · nex_lab.rooms · worker-food · food-postgres · food-adapter · nex.food_business · category-registry · domain-classifier) converges on `food` as the canonical Domain slug. Reconciled architecturally · not linguistically.

**Enforcement implications (doctrine · not implemented by this ADR):**

- Guardian rule: any read/write of a Domain-typed field carrying `food-beverage` or `food_beverage` is canonically interpreted as Domain `food` · but never silently rewritten in physical storage (aliases documented · originals preserved)
- Guardian rule: use of `food-beverage` at LAM Object Type layer OR at Category layer where the intent is Domain-shaped is a G-2 non-bypass violation
- No physical row modified · no migration authored · no CHECK constraint added by this decision
- Reversibility path preserved: a future founder-authorised ADR MAY promote `food-beverage` to a distinct Domain if a business boundary arises

### 13.9 · Reasoning under architectural-boundary frame (added 2026-09-11 · post founder clarification)

**Founder clarification 2026-09-11 verbatim:**

> *"Do not decide Food vs Food-Beverage based on general linguistic assumptions. Reconcile the existing NEX taxonomy and determine whether NEX already treats them as separate subject domains. Specifically inspect: existing Domain enums · NexVertical definitions · nex_lab.rooms · accommodation/activity taxonomies · food-related knowledge structures · marketplace/product structures · existing intent registries · existing database constraints and seeds · any documented NEX domain taxonomy. Determine whether Food, Food-Beverage, or both already have an established architectural meaning. If they represent genuinely different subject boundaries, recommend distinct Domains. If food-beverage is merely another name for Food in existing NEX architecture, recommend Food as canonical with food-beverage as an alias."*
>
> Founder also observed that NEX already carries a broad real-world subject taxonomy including: Food · Hotels · Accommodation · Products · Rentals · Cultural · History · Places · Locations · Activities · Transport · Business · Attractions · Services · and other domains — spanning multiple tiers (some of these are Domains, some are Categories within Domains; this ADR does not lock those relationships).

The recommendation in §13.6 is REAFFIRMED under this architectural frame · with reasoning restated below to make the architectural (not linguistic) basis explicit.

**Question restated: does NEX's existing architecture treat `food-beverage` as a distinct SUBJECT-DOMAIN boundary from `food`?**

Systematic re-inspection of every source enumerated by the founder:

| Source (founder's list) | Presence of `food` | Presence of `food-beverage` | Distinct-subject-boundary evidence? |
|---|---|---|---|
| Existing Domain enums (`NexVertical`) | YES · `type NexVertical = "food" \| "accommodation" \| "rentals"` | NO · not a NexVertical | ❌ · food-beverage is NOT a peer of food at Domain enum layer |
| `nex_lab.rooms` seed (10 rooms) | YES · `('food', 'Food Lab', 'nex_lab_food', ...)` seeded PK | NO · no `food-beverage` room · no `beverage` room | ❌ · food-beverage is NOT a peer Lab room |
| Accommodation activity-taxonomy (11 groups · 87 slugs) | Indirect: `FOOD_EXPERIENCE` group exists as an accommodation Category · 3 slugs (cooking_class · food_tour · tasting_experience) | NO · no `food-beverage`-group | ❌ · food-beverage is NOT enumerated as a peer accommodation activity group |
| Food-related knowledge structures (`nex.food_business` · `worker-food` · `food-adapter` · `food-postgres`) | YES · every specialist substrate keyed on `food` | NO · no `food-beverage`-business table · no `worker-food-beverage` · no `food-beverage-adapter` | ❌ · food-beverage has no specialist substrate |
| Marketplace/product structures (`nex.mp_category`) | Not present in mp_category seed | YES (underscore variant) · `('food_beverage', 'Food & Beverage', 130)` · one of 19 marketplace product categories | 🟡 · food_beverage exists at MARKETPLACE PRODUCT CATEGORY layer · orthogonal to NEX Intelligence Domain (marketplace product ≠ knowledge subject-matter) · this is a §7.5 concern, not evidence for a distinct Intelligence Domain |
| Existing intent registries (accommodation intent-registry) | Indirect: `nearby_food` intent · tokens `["restaurant", "food", "cafe", "eat"]` | NO · no `nearby_food_beverage` intent | ❌ · no distinct intent surface for food-beverage |
| Domain classifier (`live-chat-completion/domain-classifier.ts`) | YES · `food: "food"` · `restaurant: "food"` · both resolve to Domain `food` | NO · no `food-beverage: "food-beverage"` mapping | ❌ · classifier treats food-related tokens as one Domain |
| Existing database constraints and seeds | `nex_lab.rooms` seed = `food` (locked · not CHECK-enforced but seeded PK) | `nex.business_lead_directory.category_group` comment-documented (NOT CHECK-enforced) as one of 6 values · `nex.mp_category.key` seeded as `food_beverage` (marketplace product category) · `nex.marketing_contact.category_group` comment-documented mirror | 🟡 · food-beverage exists at BUSINESS-CLASSIFICATION tier · not at Domain tier |
| Business-lead routing (`business-lead-executor.ts:43`) | Target · food-beverage rows route TO `nex.food_business` (the food specialist) | Source · food-beverage rows are ROUTED · not terminal | ❌ · the code EXPLICITLY establishes that food-beverage-classified rows belong to the food Domain · they terminate in the food specialist substrate |
| Documented NEX domain taxonomy | ADR-0033 brains list (11 image-brains): staircase · door · interior · kitchen · bathroom · tools · timber · flooring · lighting · roofing · marketing · none are `food` or `food-beverage` (that list is trades-vertical only) | NO | 🟡 · ADR-0033 does not enumerate food domains at all · out of that scope · not evidence either way |
| Lab categoriser operational logs | Indirect · Lab classifier assigns compound slugs like `food-beverage/warung-local` · terminates in food-related business types (fast-food-chain · warung-local · coffee-cafe · restaurant · bakery · bar-nightlife) | YES · used as category_group parent | ❌ · every compound slug describes a food-service business type · no non-food usage of food-beverage observed |

**Architectural conclusion under the founder's frame:**

- **`food` HAS an established architectural meaning as a Domain in NEX** · confirmed by NexVertical enum · nex_lab.rooms · dedicated business table · dedicated worker · dedicated Brain adapter · dedicated Chat adapter · classifier target · category-registry entry.
- **`food-beverage` DOES NOT have an established architectural meaning as a distinct Domain in NEX** · no peer status at any Domain-layer surface · no specialist substrate · no distinct classifier target · no dedicated worker.
- **`food-beverage` DOES have an established architectural meaning at the business-classification tier** · used to group businesses within `nex.business_lead_directory` and `nex.marketing_contact` · but rows tagged this way route TO the food Domain specialist (`nex.food_business`).
- **`food_beverage` (underscore variant) in `nex.mp_category`** is a marketplace product category · orthogonal to NEX Intelligence Domain taxonomy (marketplace is a distinct logical layer · not evidence for a distinct Intelligence Domain).

**In NEX's existing architecture: `food-beverage` is not a distinct subject boundary from `food`. It functions as a business-classification-tier label whose subject scope is the food Domain.**

This satisfies the founder's condition: *"If food-beverage is merely another name for Food in existing NEX architecture, recommend Food as canonical with food-beverage as an alias."*

**Recommendation reaffirmed on architectural grounds:** Candidate wording A1 (concise · addresses underscore variant · addresses Category-tier interpretation · addresses marketplace orthogonality · addresses anti-substitution). Not linguistic. Not aesthetic. Architectural.

**Founder directive verbatim 2026-09-11:**

Resolve whether the following values are: NEX subject-matter Domains · operational-scope values that must NOT enter the Domain taxonomy · a separate architectural axis · or genuinely mixed cases requiring individual treatment. ... Distinguish rigorously between: Subject-matter Domain (what NEX knows about) · Operational scope (what NEX does · processes · displays · routes · generates · or operates) · Agent/runtime identity (who/what performs work) · Separate architectural axis (a classification dimension that is neither Domain nor LAM Object Type)."*

Values to classify: **image · news · voice · chat · monetization · marketing · activities · master_ai**.

Doctrine-only reconciliation. Preserving §7.1 §7.2 evidence unchanged.

**Architectural finding from cross-reference:**

- Values that appear as `AgentId` but NOT in domain-classifier are AGENT/RUNTIME identities · not subject-matter Domains: `master_ai` · `speaking` · `vision` · `programmer`
- Values that appear in `nex_lab.rooms` but NOT in domain-classifier AND NOT in AgentId are OPERATIONAL LAB SCOPES: `image` · `news` · `voice` · `chat` · `monetization` (all 5 of these have "Lab" in display_name and operational purpose statements)
- The `activities` Lab room is an OUTLIER — it's Lab-only, promotes into an existing Domain's specialist table (`attractions`), and has subject-matter content (tours · rentals · experiences)

### 14.3 · Per-value evidence detail

**14.3.1 · `image`**

Existing usage: purely OPERATIONAL. `nex_lab_image` Lab schema harvests OSM+Wikimedia images (500K target); image manifest (ADR-0024) records each image with a `subject_domain` field — proving that images CARRY a subject-matter Domain, they are never the subject themselves. ADR-0028/0029/0030/0033 explicitly frame image work as "IMAGE → KNOWLEDGE → MEMORY → RELATIONSHIPS → ...", with the input being the image and the OUTPUT being knowledge on some subject-matter Domain.

Architectural role: **operational scope** (image processing infrastructure). The agent-runtime cognate is `vision` (AgentId), which is what NEX DOES with images, not what NEX KNOWS. If image became a Domain, the classifier would need `image: "image"` and rows would need to distinguish "an image about a staircase" from "the staircase" — this conflates the delivery/asset layer with the knowledge layer, violating R-DOMAIN-01.

Master AI recommendation (labelled): image is NOT a NEX Domain. It is an operational scope.

**14.3.2 · `news`**

Existing usage: purely OPERATIONAL. `nex_lab_news` Lab schema harvests Kompas · Detik · Antara RSS · target 100K. Purpose: *"topic extraction · demand signal contribution"* — news feeds are used to EXTRACT topics that then flow to other Domains (accommodation demand · food trends · etc.). News-as-source ≠ News-as-subject.

Architectural role: **operational scope** (feed harvesting for signal extraction). No `worker-news` · no `news-adapter` · no `news-classifier-domain`.

Master AI recommendation (labelled): news is NOT a NEX Domain. It is an operational scope for demand-signal harvesting.

**14.3.3 · `voice`**

Existing usage: OPERATIONAL Lab (`nex_lab_voice`, STT/TTS benchmark corpus, target 10K) + AGENT cognate `speaking` (AgentId type). Phase 12 Voice Foundations ships /api/nex/voice/{transcribe,synthesize} endpoints; the voice pipeline transports text to/from user via audio but does not itself carry subject-matter.

Architectural role: **operational scope** (Lab benchmark) AND **agent/runtime identity** (via `speaking` AgentId which performs voice work). Neither is a subject-matter Domain — a user speaking "how do I install a handrail?" has Domain=staircase-adjacent (handrail-handling); voice is only the CHANNEL.

Master AI recommendation (labelled): voice is NOT a NEX Domain. It is an operational scope with an agent-identity cognate `speaking`.

**14.3.4 · `chat`**

Existing usage: OPERATIONAL Lab (`nex_lab_chat`, A/B tone profiles · prompt variants · target 100K). The `live-chat-completion` module is a large delivery-surface subsystem but its contents consume Domain knowledge from other Domains (accommodation adapter · food adapter · etc.) — chat is a delivery mode, not a knowledge subject.

Architectural role: **operational scope** (chat surface tuning + conversation ledger). No chat-classifier-domain.

Master AI recommendation (labelled): chat is NOT a NEX Domain. It is an operational delivery-surface scope.

**14.3.5 · `monetization`**

Existing usage: OPERATIONAL Lab (`nex_lab_monetization`, target 0 · "6 doctrine-safe revenue models · pilot flows · unit economics"). Marketplace pricing lives in a separate marketplace substrate (`nex.mp_*`); monetization Lab is a modelling scope for revenue experiments.

Architectural role: **operational scope** (revenue-model experimentation). No monetization-adapter · no monetization-classifier-domain.

Master AI recommendation (labelled): monetization is NOT a NEX Domain. It is an operational business-logic scope.

**14.3.6 · `marketing` — 🟡 MIXED CASE**

Existing usage is genuinely mixed:

- **Subject-matter context 1 (Business Brain):** ADR-0021 lists `Marketing` alongside `Estimator · Bookkeeper · Operations · Sales · Compliance` as a Business Brain — a legitimate business-knowledge subject-matter with a Product Lead as owner.
- **Subject-matter context 2 (Image Brain):** ADR-0033 lists `marketing` in the 11-brain image-brain isolation list — an image-processing brain scoped to marketing collateral (marketing visuals · brand assets · promotional banners). This is subject-matter at a specific tier (asset intelligence per brand/marketing scope).
- **Operational context 1 (campaigns/email/notifications):** 15+ code files in `campaigns/` · `email/` · `notifications/` · `contacts/` · `analytics/` reference `marketing` as an operational feature area (email compliance · campaign categorisation · notification classification · consent management).
- **Operational context 2 (Lab):** `data/nex-lab/marketing-import*.log` files exist — some Lab marketing operational activity, though marketing is NOT in the seeded `nex_lab.rooms` list.

Architectural role: MIXED. Marketing subject-matter (Business Brain knowledge · marketing image assets) IS potentially a Domain candidate; marketing operations (campaign delivery · email · notifications) are NOT Domain-shaped.

Founder's own directive (2026-09-11) recognised this: *"marketing needs careful inspection because it could contain genuine subject-matter knowledge as well as an operational function."*

Master AI recommendation (labelled): `marketing` is a **mixed case requiring individual treatment**. Provisional split proposal for founder authorisation:

- `marketing` (subject-matter) — as a candidate Domain covering Marketing knowledge/brand knowledge/best-practices (aligns with ADR-0021 Business Brain scope · aligns with ADR-0033 image-brain scope)
- `marketing` (operational) — the campaigns/email/notifications usage remains outside the Domain taxonomy · lives in an operational-scope axis

Founder may alternatively decide:

- (a) marketing is fully operational · not a Domain (defers subject-matter marketing knowledge to a future ADR)
- (b) marketing is fully a Domain · operational usages are Guardian-rejected as anti-substitution (would break existing code · disruptive)
- (c) split as above (recommended)

**14.3.7 · `activities` — 🟡 CANDIDATE SUBJECT-MATTER · DOMAIN TIER TBD**

Existing usage is more subject-matter-shaped than the pure operational scopes:

- Lab room purpose (`nex_lab.rooms.purpose = "Tours · bike rentals · scooter rentals · surf lessons · cultural experiences"`) describes real subject-matter content · things a NEX user does at a destination.
- Lab display name is "Activities & Rentals Lab" — pairs `activities` with `rentals` (a NexVertical) in one Lab.
- `activities-executor.ts` promotes verified Lab rows into `nex.brain_attractions` — the target is `attractions` (a domain-classifier Domain, though not itself a Lab room).
- Accommodation `activity-taxonomy.ts` enumerates 11 activity groups + 87 activity type slugs — but scoped to Accommodation Category tier (accommodation nearby-relationships), not a top-level activities Domain.
- No `worker-activities` · no `activities-adapter` · no `activities-classifier-domain` · no `nex.activities_business`.

Architectural role: **the subject-matter content of "activities" DOES exist**, but architecturally it flows into `attractions` (existing Domain) OR appears as a Category taxonomy within Accommodation. `activities` itself may be:

- (a) a **Category-tier label** within an `attractions` Domain (i.e. activities-you-do-at-attractions) — consistent with promotion target
- (b) a **distinct Domain** paired with attractions (e.g. attractions = places to visit · activities = things to do at/from those places)
- (c) a **Lab operational label** only · not any part of the canonical taxonomy

Master AI recommendation (labelled): defer canonical classification of `activities` to `attractions` Domain reconciliation (a future §7.x item). For this ADR, classify `activities` as:

- **Provisionally an operational Lab scope** (aligns with §7.3 discipline)
- **With a candidate Category interpretation** within `attractions` Domain OR with `attractions`
- **Not folded silently into Accommodation** despite the accommodation activity-taxonomy overlap — that taxonomy is Category-scoped to Accommodation, not authority over top-level Activities semantics

Founder may alternatively decide: promote `activities` to a distinct Domain, at which point ADR-0314a.2.s.attractions or a similar companion must reconcile activities ↔ attractions.

**14.3.8 · `master_ai`**

Existing usage: explicit **agent/runtime identity**. AgentId comment in `src/lib/nex/agent-runtime/types.ts:29-32` states verbatim: *"master_ai (Wave-3): the observer/intelligence daemon. Its worker is worker-master-ai.ts and its runtime is governed by the same heartbeat/PID/founder-stop discipline as the two specialist agents. READ-ONLY against every other agent."*

Master AI observes the runtime · never carries subject-matter · never routes knowledge. Its worker is `worker-master-ai.ts`; its module `src/lib/nex/master-ai/*` includes agent-registry · claude-session-observer · endurance-report · master-ai (tests) · observation-wave-s-coverage · self-criticism etc.

Architectural role: **agent/runtime identity** — the axis founder specifically named.

Master AI recommendation (labelled): master_ai is NOT a NEX Domain. It is an **agent/runtime identity** (the fourth axis the founder proposed alongside LAM Object Type · Domain · Category).

### 14.4 · Consequences of each classification (per bucket)

**Bucket · Operational scope non-Domain:**

Applies to: `image` · `news` · `voice` · `chat` · `monetization` (5 values). If declared non-Domain:

- ✅ Preserves Domain taxonomy as subject-matter-only
- ✅ Lab room registry continues to function unchanged (rooms remain operational Lab units · not knowledge Domain slots)
- ✅ Aligns with R-DOMAIN-01 (Domain is subject-matter · not operational-scope)
- ⚠️ Requires a canonical name for the axis (e.g. "Operational Scope") · authored in a future ADR (§7.3 flags this as escalation-worthy)
- ⚠️ Guardian rejects use of these values in Domain-shaped fields
- Reversibility: HIGH · a future founder ADR may promote any of these to a Domain if subject-matter emerges (e.g. if NEX develops chat-as-a-knowledge-subject research)

**Bucket · Agent/runtime identity:**

Applies to: `master_ai` (plus retroactively `speaking` · `vision` · `programmer` · but those are §7.6 alias questions). If declared agent/runtime identity:

- ✅ Preserves existing `AgentId` type as the authority for agent-identity axis
- ✅ Aligns with Master AI's role as READ-ONLY observer (explicit in code)
- ⚠️ Guardian rejects use of `master_ai` in Domain fields
- ⚠️ Formalises "Agent/Runtime Identity" as a fourth architectural axis (LAM Object Type · Domain · Category · Agent/Runtime Identity) — this IS a genuinely-new axis · §7.10 escalation-worthy

**Bucket · Mixed case (marketing):**

Applies to: `marketing`. Requires split treatment (subject-matter Marketing Brain + operational marketing). Consequence:

- Guardian must know which layer the value is being used at when validating writes
- Splits into two doctrines: "Marketing (subject-matter Domain candidate · authored in future ADR)" AND "marketing (operational · not a Domain)"
- Reversibility: HIGH

**Bucket · Candidate subject-matter (activities):**

Applies to: `activities`. Consequence depends on the choice:

- (a) Category-tier under `attractions` — preserves attractions Domain · activities becomes a Category label · needs Category taxonomy ADR to author sub-values
- (b) Distinct Domain paired with `attractions` — creates a new Domain · deferred to future ADR that reconciles activities/attractions/rentals
- (c) Lab-operational-only — leaves the subject-matter content orphaned; would need a different Domain to receive `nex.brain_attractions` rows (which is exactly `attractions`)

**Bucket · Separate architectural axis:**

If founder authorises formal recognition of two new axes:

- **Operational Scope axis** — holds image · news · voice · chat · monetization (and future operational-only values)
- **Agent/Runtime Identity axis** — holds master_ai (and retroactively the AgentId type members)

This is the cleanest architectural answer per R-DOMAIN-01's spirit (no dimension may be silently substituted for another).

### 14.5 · Master AI recommendation (labelled · not binding · founder decides)

**Recommendation summary (per bucket):**

| Value | Recommended architectural bucket |
|---|---|
| **image** | Operational Scope · non-Domain |
| **news** | Operational Scope · non-Domain |
| **voice** | Operational Scope · non-Domain (with `speaking` agent-cognate) |
| **chat** | Operational Scope · non-Domain |
| **monetization** | Operational Scope · non-Domain |
| **marketing** | 🟡 Mixed case · propose split: subject-matter `marketing` deferred as Domain candidate (future ADR) · operational `marketing` non-Domain |
| **activities** | 🟡 Candidate Category-tier under `attractions` Domain (deferred to future §7.x that reconciles attractions/activities/rentals) · treat as operational Lab-harvest scope for §7.3 purposes |
| **master_ai** | Agent/Runtime Identity · non-Domain (formalises fourth axis) |

**Grounds:**

- Every operational-scope value has ZERO Domain-layer surface (no classifier · no AgentId · no NexVertical · no specialist adapter/worker/table).
- Every operational-scope value has PURPOSE STATEMENTS that describe what NEX DOES with data · not what NEX KNOWS about.
- Master_ai has explicit code-level comment declaring itself an observer/daemon agent · not a subject.
- Marketing is genuinely mixed (ADR-0021 · ADR-0033 · vs campaigns/email operational usage) · needs individual treatment.
- Activities has real subject-matter content but that content already routes to the `attractions` Domain via existing executor code · so its canonical positioning waits on attractions reconciliation.

**Decision provenance footer:**

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Decision** | B1 · five-axis architecture locked · image · news · voice · chat · monetization = Operational Scope · master_ai = Agent/Runtime Identity · marketing = mixed case (operational scope + deferred subject-matter Domain candidate) · activities = deferred to attractions reconciliation (candidate Category under attractions Domain) · anti-substitution enforced across all axes |
| **Decided by** | Philip |

**Load-bearing architectural basis:**

- Every operational-scope value (`image` · `news` · `voice` · `chat` · `monetization`) has ZERO Domain-layer surface (no classifier · no AgentId · no NexVertical · no specialist adapter/worker/table) · every one has an operational purpose statement in `src/lib/nex/lab/rooms.ts`.
- `master_ai` is verbatim declared in `src/lib/nex/agent-runtime/types.ts:29-32` as *"the observer/intelligence daemon · READ-ONLY against every other agent"* — an agent/runtime identity, not a knowledge subject.
- `marketing` presence in ADR-0021 Business Brains + ADR-0033 image-brains establishes legitimate subject-matter usage; presence in 15+ campaigns/email/notifications files establishes operational usage. Split is architecturally necessary.
- `activities-executor.ts` promotes verified rows into `nex.brain_attractions` — activities-content routes into the existing `attractions` Domain, so `activities` positioning waits on attractions reconciliation (out of §7.3 scope).

**Five-axis architecture (post-B1 · locked):**

The NEX logical taxonomy now recognises **five orthogonal architectural axes**:

| # | Axis | Answers | Established authority |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **LAM Object Type** | *What KIND of NEX logical object is this?* | ADR-0309.1 · 13 types + Bridge (from ADR-0312) = 14 |
| 2 | **Domain** | *What SUBJECT does this object concern?* | R-DOMAIN-01.v1.0.0 (locked 2026-09-11) · canonical values authored in ADR-0314a.2.s and its companion ADRs |
| 3 | **Category** | *How is that subject sub-classified?* | R-DOMAIN-01.v1.0.0 · per-Domain (or shared per founder authorisation) · versioned separately |
| 4 | **Operational Scope** | *What OPERATIONAL AREA of NEX does this belong to?* | §7.3.B1.v1.0.0 (locked 2026-09-11) · canonical values authored in future companion ADR-0314a.2.t |
| 5 | **Agent/Runtime Identity** | *WHICH NEX agent/process is responsible for this work?* | §7.3.B1.v1.0.0 (locked 2026-09-11) · `AgentId` type in `src/lib/nex/agent-runtime/types.ts` provisionally authoritative pending ADR-0314a.2.u |

**Anti-substitution rule extended:** R-DOMAIN-01 forbids substitution among any of the five axes. Guardian rejects a value from one axis used in a field typed for another axis (G-2 non-bypass violation).

**Enforcement implications (doctrine · not implemented by this ADR):**

- Guardian rule: any Domain-typed field carrying `image` · `news` · `voice` · `chat` · `monetization` · `master_ai` is a G-2 violation
- Guardian rule: any Operational Scope-typed field carrying subject-matter Domain slugs (accommodation · food · staircase · etc.) is a G-2 violation
- Guardian rule: any Agent/Runtime Identity-typed field carrying Domain slugs OR Operational Scope slugs is a G-2 violation
- Marketing at operational-scope fields is permitted (operational marketing) · marketing at Domain-typed fields is rejected pending future founder-authorised Marketing Domain ADR
- Activities at Domain-typed fields is rejected pending attractions-reconciliation ADR
- No physical row modified · no CHECK constraint added · no code change made by this decision
- Reversibility path preserved: future founder ADR may promote any of these values to a Domain if subject-matter emerges (e.g. Marketing Domain · Activities Domain)

**Companion ADRs generated by this decision (pending founder authorship):**

- **ADR-0314a.2.t** · Operational Scope Axis · canonical values authored here (start: image · news · voice · chat · monetization · additional operational values as needed)
- **ADR-0314a.2.u** · Agent/Runtime Identity Axis · canonical values authored here (start: master_ai · retroactively covering AgentId type members pending §7.6 alias resolution)
- Future ADR (candidate slot) · Marketing subject-matter Domain candidate (deferred from §7.3 · authored only if founder authorises)
- Future ADR (candidate slot · attractions companion) · reconciles activities · attractions · rentals
