## Section 15 · §7.4 evidence + candidate resolution · 2026-09-11

**Founder directive verbatim 2026-09-11:**

> *"AUTHORISE §7.4 ARCHITECTURAL INSPECTION ONLY. Master AI may inspect the 11 activity groups and 87 activity slugs across all relevant code, database schemas, existing data, Lab executors, specialist brains, routing, intents, and storage. Determine whether the activity taxonomy is: A — Category within Accommodation only · B — Shared cross-domain Category with applicable_domains · C — Split taxonomy where individual activity groups belong to different Domains. Preserve all existing evidence. No migration, no rows changed, no taxonomy values invented, no code changes, no Guardian changes. Produce the evidence and founder decision candidates only. Gate 3 remains CLOSED. STOP after the inspection and await Philip's decision."*

Doctrine-only inspection. Preserving §7.1 §7.2 §7.3 evidence unchanged.

### 15.1 · Author-declared purpose (from `activity-taxonomy.ts` header · lines 6-9)

The file itself declares its architectural role verbatim:

> *"NEX Accommodation Agent · Activity / Destination Taxonomy (Rule Book v3) · Founder BEGIN AUTHORIZATION 2026-09-08 · Indonesia Complete Intelligence Mission. This module implements the rule-book upgrade the Founder authorized in the 37-section directive. **Accommodation is no longer just 'properties' — it is the anchor from which travellers reach food · drink · nature · culture · walks · activities · attractions · transport** (§4 · §5 · §25 · §35)."*
>
> *"This file defines the CONTRACT (types · enums · registries · aliases). It does NOT collect anything, does NOT wire to chat, does NOT activate any worker. Collection · composition · observatory · gap-engine activation are deferred to separate Founder BEGINs per §22 · §36 hard-stop discipline."*

**Architectural claim from the author-declared purpose:** the activity taxonomy is authored INSIDE the accommodation module but explicitly serves as a **cross-Domain anchor** — accommodation is the source anchor, and the taxonomy reaches OUTWARD to `food · nature · culture · walks · activities · attractions · transport`. This is prima facie evidence that the taxonomy is NOT Category-within-Accommodation-only (Option A) · it reaches multiple Domains by its own declared purpose.

### 15.2 · 10-source inspection matrix (compact)

| Source | Presence of activity taxonomy | Interpretation |
|---|:---:|---|
| domain-classifier (`domain-classifier.ts`) | ❌ — the 11 groups + 87 slugs are NOT in the classifier's DOMAIN_TRIGGERS enum | Activity taxonomy does NOT function as a top-level Domain classifier target |
| AgentId (`agent-runtime/types.ts`) | ❌ — no `activities` agent · but adjacent agents `travel · vision · construction · healthcare` exist | Activities is not an Agent/Runtime Identity per §7.3 |
| NexVertical (`category-registry.ts`) | ❌ — 3 verticals: food · accommodation · rentals · rentals shares Lab display name with activities | Activities is not a NexVertical · but paired with rentals in Lab |
| `nex_lab.rooms` seed | ✅ · `('activities', 'Activities & Rentals Lab', 'nex_lab_activities', 'lab_harvest_activities', ARRAY['osm','tourism_boards'], 50000)` | Activities has its own Lab room · display "Activities & Rentals Lab" · target 50K |
| `nex_lab_activities.*` (Postgres) | ✅ · harvest_raw 2,342 rows · verified 780 rows (per §7.1-audit data) | Lab produces verified activity data |
| Specialist brain table | 🟡 · activities-executor promotes into `nex.brain_attractions` NOT a `nex.brain_activities` table (though `nex.brain_activities` schema exists with 0 rows) | Existing routing conflates activities → attractions at the executor layer |
| `activities-executor.ts` | ✅ · function `attractionKind(name, rawTags)` translates activity harvest into 12-value `attraction_kind` CHECK enum: `temple · beach · waterfall · mountain · national_park · museum · monument · historical_site · viewpoint · market · village · other` | Only a SUBSET of the 87 activity slugs map cleanly to `attraction_kind` · rest fall through to `other` |
| Intent registries (accommodation) | ✅ · `nearby_attractions` (tokens: attraction · sights) · `nearby_transport` (tokens: transport · bus · train · station) · `nearby_food` (§7.2 already covered) — accommodation intents reach OUTWARD to Attractions · Transport · Food · Attractions Domain values | Accommodation intents themselves treat these as CROSS-DOMAIN targets · not accommodation-internal |
| Activity taxonomy file scope | Authored inside `src/lib/nex/intelligence-storage-grid/accommodation/` | Historical scoping · author-declared purpose (see §15.1) says it's an anchor · not a purely-scoped Category |
| Cross-domain references | Other `intelligence-storage-grid` folders (food · transport · etc.) do NOT exist yet — accommodation is the only ISG module currently authored · so the activity taxonomy sits there de facto | Accommodation module is the AUTHOR of the cross-domain anchor · but the anchor points to other Domains |
| ADR-authored subject-matter | ADR-0304 (NEX Lab) + Founder BEGIN 2026-09-08 rule-book v3 · §5 explicitly names the 9-group taxonomy as the "activity groups" — treated as cross-domain per §25 relationship discipline | Founder's original BEGIN framed it as anchor-based cross-domain · not accommodation-internal |
| Row counts (from audit) | 780 Lab-verified rows · 32 brain_attractions rows | 748 verified rows NOT promoted to attractions · direct evidence that many activity slugs DON'T fit attractions Domain shape |

### 15.3 · Per-group Domain-fit analysis (evidence-based · no locking)

For each of the 11 activity groups, cross-reference the slugs against existing Domains:

| Group | Slugs (verbatim from activity-taxonomy.ts) | Predominant shape | Best-fit existing Domain(s) |
|---|---|---|---|
| **NATURE** (14) | lake · waterfall · river · beach · mountain · volcano · forest · jungle · cave · island · coral_reef · hot_spring · rice_terrace · viewpoint · sunrise_location · sunset_location | Places (physical geography) | **Attractions Domain** (most fit `attraction_kind` enum · beach · mountain · waterfall · viewpoint) · candidate hot_spring/volcano/coral_reef would fall to `other` |
| **OUTDOOR** (18) | hiking · walking · trekking · cycling · running · swimming · snorkelling · diving · surfing · fishing · kayaking · rafting · camping · horse_riding · golf · climbing · wildlife_watching · bird_watching | **Verbs · things you DO** — not places | 🟡 **DOES NOT fit any existing Domain cleanly** · these are activity-verbs · candidate for a distinct Activities Domain if founder authorises OR Category-tier within Attractions |
| **CULTURE** (12) | temple · mosque · church · palace · museum · historic_site · traditional_village · cultural_performance · festival · market · craft_centre · heritage_area | Places (some events: cultural_performance · festival) | **Attractions Domain** (temple · museum · palace · historic_site · market · village map to `attraction_kind` cleanly) · cultural_performance and festival are EVENTS not places · orphan |
| **ENTERTAINMENT** (3) | night_market · cinema · entertainment_venue | Places | **Attractions Domain** (market · other) partially fits |
| **WELLNESS** (3) | spa · wellness_centre · thermal_bath | Places | 🟡 **NO CLEAN FIT** in existing Domains · candidate Business/Services Domain (spa business) OR Attractions Domain (thermal_bath as destination) · could be Health-adjacent (healthcare AgentId exists but no brain substrate) |
| **FOOD_EXPERIENCE** (3) | cooking_class · food_tour · tasting_experience | Verbs · things you DO around food | **Food Domain** (§7.2 A1 locked) · these are activity-verbs within Food subject-matter |
| **SHOPPING** (3) | shopping_mall · shopping_street · souvenir_shop | Places (retail businesses) | **Business Domain** (retail-shaped) · OR Marketplace substrate (nex.mp_*) |
| **NIGHTLIFE** (3) | bar · nightclub · live_music_venue | Places (evening businesses) | **Business Domain** (bar · nightclub are food-beverage businesses per §7.2 A1 · food-beverage aliases food) · OR Entertainment sub-Category |
| **TRANSPORT_HUB** (10) | airport · rail_station · bus_station · ferry_terminal · metro_station · tram_stop · taxi_rank · ride_pickup_zone · parking · ev_charging | Places (transport infrastructure) | **Transport Domain** (clearly · transport is an established Domain per §7.3 audit) |
| **SERVICE** (7) | atm · pharmacy · clinic · hospital · laundry · post_office · consulate | Places (service businesses) | 🟡 **Split**: `pharmacy · clinic · hospital` → Healthcare (AgentId exists · no brain yet) · `atm · laundry · post_office · consulate` → Business/Services Domain |
| **OTHER** (0 slugs) | Sentinel | — | Sentinel — §7.7 escalation |

**Findings from per-group analysis:**

- Only **OUTDOOR (18 slugs)** and **cultural events (2 of CULTURE's 12: cultural_performance · festival)** clearly do NOT fit any existing Domain — they are **activity-verbs** (things you DO) rather than places.
- **NATURE (14) · CULTURE (12) · ENTERTAINMENT (3)** predominantly fit the existing **Attractions** Domain (as places-to-visit).
- **TRANSPORT_HUB (10)** cleanly fits the existing **Transport** Domain.
- **FOOD_EXPERIENCE (3)** cleanly fits the existing **Food** Domain.
- **SHOPPING · NIGHTLIFE (6)** predominantly fit **Business** Domain (retail/hospitality businesses).
- **SERVICE (7)** splits between Healthcare (3) and Business/Services (4).
- **WELLNESS (3)** has no clean home yet (candidate escalation).

**~70 of 87 slugs map to existing Domains via straightforward Domain fit. ~17 slugs (mostly OUTDOOR verbs + a few events + WELLNESS) do NOT have a clean home in current Domains.**

### 15.4 · Cross-referencing with `nex.brain_attractions.attraction_kind` CHECK constraint

The current substrate constraint (per `activities-executor.ts` line 27-29):

> `'temple' · 'beach' · 'waterfall' · 'mountain' · 'national_park' · 'museum' · 'monument' · 'historical_site' · 'viewpoint' · 'market' · 'village' · 'other'`

Cross-referenced against the 87 activity slugs:

- **Clean maps (11 slugs · 12.6% of taxonomy):** temple · beach · waterfall · mountain · viewpoint (from NATURE) · museum · monument · historical_site → historic_site (from CULTURE) · market (from CULTURE) · traditional_village → village (from CULTURE)
- **Partial-match maps (~5-8 slugs):** volcano→mountain · hot_spring→other · rice_terrace→other · palace→monument (arguable) · night_market→market
- **No map (60+ slugs):** all 18 OUTDOOR verbs · all 10 TRANSPORT_HUB · all 7 SERVICE · all 3 SHOPPING · all 3 NIGHTLIFE · all 3 FOOD_EXPERIENCE · all 3 WELLNESS · most ENTERTAINMENT (cinema · entertainment_venue) · a few CULTURE (mosque · church · cultural_performance · festival · craft_centre · heritage_area)

**Substrate evidence:** the current attraction_kind CHECK covers only ~12% of the activity taxonomy cleanly. Rest fall through to `other` (per `attractionKind()` fallback line 46). This is direct architectural evidence that a large fraction of the activity taxonomy is being LOST in the current attractions-only pipeline.

**Verification via audit numbers:** `nex_lab_activities.verified` **780 rows** vs `nex.brain_attractions` **32 rows** — 748 verified rows not promoted. Some of that gap is dedup-by-(location_slug,name_en) OR promotion cycle limit · but a meaningful fraction is likely dropped or collapsed to "other" because the taxonomy-to-attraction_kind translation is lossy.

### 15.5 · Consequences of each candidate

**Consequence Table A · Category within Accommodation only:**

| Consequence | Impact |
|---|---|
| Doctrine · activity taxonomy is Accommodation-scoped Category | 🔴 CONTRADICTS the author-declared purpose (activity-taxonomy.ts header verbatim declares it as a cross-domain anchor) |
| Doctrine · Transport hub queries (`nearby_transport`) treat transport as accommodation Category | 🔴 Fights the Transport Domain (existing · classifier + AgentId + Lab room) — creates alias/synonym conflict per §7.2 anti-substitution rule |
| Doctrine · Food experiences treated as Accommodation Category not Food Category | 🔴 Fights the Food Domain (§7.2 A1 · food canonical) — same anti-substitution conflict |
| Doctrine · Service (pharmacy/clinic/hospital) treated as Accommodation Category | 🔴 No clean fit · pharmacies are not accommodation-specific |
| Fragmentation risk | 🔴 HIGH · other Domains cannot legitimately reference the taxonomy without going through Accommodation |
| Reversibility | LOW · once locked as accommodation-Category, migrating to Domain-scoped would require unwiring accommodation Category surfaces |
| Substrate impact | None immediate (doctrine only) |

**Consequence Table B · Shared cross-domain Category with applicable_domains:**

| Consequence | Impact |
|---|---|
| Doctrine · activity taxonomy is a SHARED Category with per-value `applicable_domains: string[]` | ✅ Aligns with author-declared cross-domain anchor role |
| Doctrine · each of the 87 slugs carries its own `applicable_domains` list | ✅ Preserves the current single-source-of-truth taxonomy · avoids splitting into per-Domain files |
| Doctrine · a slug like `airport` has `applicable_domains: ["transport"]`; `temple` has `["attractions", "accommodation"]` (attractions Domain owns the subject · accommodation uses as nearby-relationship) | ✅ Explicit and honest · reflects how the taxonomy already works |
| Doctrine · OUTDOOR verbs (hiking · surfing) are still-orphan; their `applicable_domains` awaits future decision | 🟡 OUTDOOR remains ambiguous · candidate Activities Domain OR Category within Attractions |
| Doctrine · Guardian rejects use of activity slug in a Domain-typed field unless the slug's `applicable_domains` contains that Domain | ✅ Enforces anti-substitution per R-DOMAIN-01 |
| Substrate impact | None immediate. Future authoring of `applicable_domains` per slug is a per-slug decision · founder authorises. |
| Reversibility | HIGH · applicable_domains lists can grow/shrink via founder ADRs |

**Consequence Table C · Split taxonomy where individual groups belong to different Domains:**

| Consequence | Impact |
|---|---|
| Doctrine · each group is REASSIGNED to a specific Domain (TRANSPORT_HUB → Transport; SERVICE-medical → Healthcare; SERVICE-other → Business; NATURE/CULTURE/ENTERTAINMENT → Attractions; FOOD_EXPERIENCE → Food; SHOPPING/NIGHTLIFE → Business; WELLNESS → orphan; OUTDOOR → candidate Activities Domain) | ✅ Aligns each slug with its natural Domain home |
| Doctrine · the single activity-taxonomy.ts file becomes deprecated in favour of per-Domain Category taxonomies | ⚠️ Requires future ADRs to author each per-Domain Category taxonomy · additional authoring surface |
| Fragmentation risk | 🟡 Medium · but the split is architecturally correct · not arbitrary |
| Reversibility | LOW · once split · reconstituting a single shared taxonomy would require re-authoring |
| Cross-domain access | Each Domain owns its subset · cross-Domain queries (`nearby_food` from accommodation reaching food) still work via Router boundary · no fundamental loss |
| Substrate impact | None immediate (doctrine-only) · future migration eventually consolidates `nex.brain_attractions` + per-Domain brain tables |

### 15.6 · Master AI recommendation (labelled · not binding · founder decides)

**Recommendation: Candidate B** (Shared cross-domain Category with `applicable_domains`).

Grounds:

1. **The author-declared purpose (from the file header itself) explicitly frames the taxonomy as a cross-Domain anchor** (`Accommodation is no longer just 'properties' — it is the anchor from which travellers reach food · drink · nature · culture · walks · activities · attractions · transport`). Option A contradicts this on the author's own terms.
2. **The current substrate already treats slugs as cross-Domain** — `nearby_attractions` reaches attractions Domain values · `nearby_transport` reaches transport Domain values · `nearby_food` (§7.2 A1) reaches food Domain values. This is the observed architecture.
3. **Option B preserves the single-source-of-truth 87-slug file** which is architecturally simpler than fragmenting into per-Domain Category taxonomies (Option C).
4. **Option B is reversible** — a future founder ADR may promote OUTDOOR verbs to a distinct Activities Domain OR split the taxonomy further (Option C-style) if a business boundary emerges.
5. **Option C's key strength — correctly assigning TRANSPORT_HUB to Transport Domain — is preserved under Option B** via each slug's `applicable_domains: ["transport"]`. Option C's per-Domain taxonomies duplicate authoring effort.
6. **Option B respects R-DOMAIN-01 anti-substitution** — the `applicable_domains` field per slug tells Guardian which Domain-typed fields may carry which slugs.
7. **The 748 verified-but-unpromoted rows in the audit are a substrate-shape mismatch problem** (activity slug ↔ 12-value attraction_kind CHECK) that Option B doesn't solve immediately, but ALSO doesn't get worse. Option C would solve it via per-Domain brain tables (transport-hub → nex.brain_transport · service → nex.brain_business_services · etc.) but requires more substrate authoring than doctrine alone can commit.

**Secondary recommendation:** If founder chooses B, also authorise a follow-up ADR (candidate slot: `ADR-0314a.2.v · Activity Slug Applicable-Domain Registry`) to author the `applicable_domains: string[]` per slug · founder-authored per slug · not invented by Master AI.

If founder prefers Option C, that also solves the split cleanly. Option C is more radical but honest about the underlying Domain-shape variance.

Option A is not recommended · it contradicts the author-declared purpose.

### 15.7 · Candidate founder decision wordings (founder authorises · amends · or writes their own)

**Candidate C1 (Option B · comprehensive):**

> *"§7.4 · The activity taxonomy authored in `src/lib/nex/intelligence-storage-grid/accommodation/activity-taxonomy.ts` (11 groups · 87 slugs) is a **shared cross-Domain Category** whose slugs may reference multiple NEX Domains via a per-slug `applicable_domains: string[]` field. The taxonomy's current filesystem scoping (under `accommodation/`) is historical (Founder BEGIN 2026-09-08 authored it inside accommodation as the first Intelligence Storage Grid module) and does not imply Accommodation ownership. The author-declared purpose (activity-taxonomy.ts header verbatim) explicitly frames the taxonomy as a cross-Domain anchor. Canonical `applicable_domains` values per slug are authored in a future founder-authorised ADR (candidate slot ADR-0314a.2.v · Activity Slug Applicable-Domain Registry) · not invented by Master AI. Guardian enforces R-DOMAIN-01 anti-substitution: a slug is acceptable in a Domain-typed field only when the target Domain appears in the slug's `applicable_domains`. Slugs whose Domain fit remains ambiguous (notably OUTDOOR verbs · WELLNESS · cultural events) remain candidates for a future distinct Activities Domain OR further split · this ADR does not lock those."*

**Candidate C2 (Option C · comprehensive):**

Per-group per-slug reassignment is authored in future founder-authorised companion ADRs · not invented by Master AI. The existing single-file taxonomy remains as a compatibility artefact; its future replacement per split is deferred to companion ADRs."*

Founder defers §7.4 resolution to a future ADR pending activities/attractions/rentals reconciliation (as flagged in §7.3-B1 activities-deferred clause). No use of activity slugs in Domain-typed fields is authorised until §7.4 resolves."*

**Master AI recommendation between C1/C2/C3 (labelled · not binding at time of proposal):**

Recommend **C1** (Option B) because it:
- Aligns with author-declared purpose
- Preserves the single-source-of-truth taxonomy file
- Uses per-slug `applicable_domains` to respect anti-substitution
- Is reversible if a future founder ADR splits (Option C) or promotes OUTDOOR to Activities Domain

C3 is preferred if founder wants to defer §7.4 entirely pending activities/attractions/rentals reconciliation (least commitment).

**🔒 FOUNDER DECISION · C1 · LOCKED 2026-09-11**

> *"§7.4 · Founder Decision: C1 — LOCKED. NEX adopts Option B: Shared cross-Domain Activity Category taxonomy with per-slug applicable_domains[]."*
>
> — Philip · 2026-09-11

**Locked doctrine (per founder's verbatim expansion):**

1. **Single governed source.** The existing activity taxonomy (`src/lib/nex/intelligence-storage-grid/accommodation/activity-taxonomy.ts` · 11 groups · 87 slugs) remains a single governed source of activity slugs.
2. **Historical scoping ≠ ownership.** Accommodation does not own the taxonomy merely because the taxonomy was initially authored inside the Accommodation module (per Founder BEGIN 2026-09-08 rule-book v3 · first ISG module authored).
3. **Per-slug `applicable_domains[]` declaration required.** Each activity slug must have a founder-authorised `applicable_domains: string[]` declaration identifying the Domain or Domains in which that activity may legitimately occur.
4. **`applicable_domains[]` is not a Domain substitute.** The `applicable_domains[]` field is a classification/relationship mechanism and MUST NOT silently substitute for the Domain axis under R-DOMAIN-01.
5. **No AI-inferred Domain assignment.** No activity slug may be assigned to a Domain by AI inference alone. Missing or ambiguous Domain applicability is **unknown/fail-closed** until governed.
6. **Explicit unresolved list (not to be invented):**
   - OUTDOOR activity Domain status (candidate distinct Activities Domain · NOT to be inferred from the ~17 orphan verbs · founder decides via future ADR)
   - Wellness Domain ownership
   - Events currently orphaned from existing Domains (cultural_performance · festival)
   - OTHER sentinel treatment (§7.7 escalation)
   - attractions / activities / rentals reconciliation (§7.4-adjacent · future founder-authorised ADR)
7. **Companion ADR authorised (spec only · not populated):**
   - **ADR-0314a.2.v · Activity Slug Applicable-Domain Registry** — created as a doctrine/authorisation specification only. Registry itself is NOT populated unless separately authorised.

**Decision provenance footer:**

|---|---|

| **Decided by** | Philip |

**Load-bearing architectural basis:**

- Author-declared purpose from `activity-taxonomy.ts` header (lines 6-9): *"Accommodation is no longer just 'properties' — it is the anchor from which travellers reach food · drink · nature · culture · walks · activities · attractions · transport."*
- Cross-domain accommodation intents already reach outward (`nearby_attractions` · `nearby_transport` · `nearby_food` § 7.2)
- 10 of 11 groups have clean Domain-fit already · only OUTDOOR verbs + WELLNESS + orphan events + OTHER sentinel are unresolved

**Enforcement implications (doctrine · not implemented by this ADR):**

- Guardian rule: an activity slug is acceptable in a Domain-typed field only when the target Domain is in the slug's founder-authorised `applicable_domains[]`
- Guardian rule: an activity slug with EMPTY or MISSING `applicable_domains[]` is fail-closed · Guardian rejects any Domain-typed write using it until governed
- G-2 non-bypass violation: use of `applicable_domains[]` as a Domain substitute
- Anti-inflation rule: the presence of ~17 orphan slugs (mostly OUTDOOR verbs) does NOT constitute evidence for an "Activities Domain" — that requires separate founder authorisation via a distinct ADR with its own architectural evidence (specialist substrate · adapter · worker · classifier · demand)
- No physical row modified · no CHECK constraint added · no code change · no activities-executor modification · no attraction_kind expansion · no brain creation
- Reversibility path preserved: future founder ADRs may (a) promote OUTDOOR to a distinct Activities Domain if evidence emerges · (b) split the taxonomy per Option C style · (c) merge into existing Domain Category tables · none of these is precluded

**Companion ADRs authored or required by this decision:**

- **ADR-0314a.2.v · Activity Slug Applicable-Domain Registry** — **Master AI authors the doctrine/authorisation specification** (schema · governance rules · fail-closed policy) · does **NOT populate** the registry with per-slug `applicable_domains` values (deferred to separate founder-authorised ADR).
- **Future ADR (candidate slot) · Activities Domain candidate** — if founder later authorises. Requires independent architectural evidence: specialist substrate · adapter · worker · classifier target · demand.
- **Future ADR (candidate slot) · Wellness Domain candidate** — same discipline.
- **Future ADR (candidate slot) · Orphan cultural events (cultural_performance · festival) reconciliation** — same discipline.
- **Future ADR (candidate slot) · attractions/activities/rentals reconciliation** — as flagged in §7.3-B1 activities-deferred clause.

## Section 16 · §7.5 evidence + candidate resolution · 2026-09-11

**Founder directive verbatim 2026-09-11:**

> *"AUTHORISE §7.5 ARCHITECTURAL INSPECTION ONLY. Audit the 12 marketplace masters, all 310 mp_category values and the 23,580 seller population across repository code, classifiers, marketplace schemas, crawlers, workers, adapters, Lab rooms, existing specialist substrates, Knowledge Router paths, NEX Chat consumption and NEX1 consumption. Determine whether Marketplace taxonomy is: A — a genuinely distinct Commerce/Marketplace Domain · B — orthogonal marketplace/product taxonomy that does not define a NEX Intelligence Domain · C — Category structure within an existing Business Domain · D — a split model requiring different treatment for sellers, products and services. Do not infer Domain existence from taxonomy size alone. Apply the locked anti-Domain-inflation doctrine from §7.4. Explicitly distinguish: seller/business identity · product · service · marketplace classification · subject-matter Domain · Category · Operational Scope · Agent/Runtime Identity. Inspect whether marketplace data is merely a commercial delivery surface or whether it represents an independent subject-matter knowledge population requiring its own Domain Brain."*

Doctrine-only inspection. Preserving §7.1 §7.2 §7.3 §7.4 evidence unchanged.

### 16.2 · Distinguishing seller · product · service · marketplace-classification (per founder directive)

| Axis | Substrate | Purpose | Cardinality |

| **Service** | `nex.service_business` (per Domain Brain Coverage Audit · **3,922 rows**) · separate substrate from mp_* | Businesses that provide services (not products) · specialist table | 3,922 rows all `status=listed · verified=false` |
| **Marketplace classification** | `nex.mp_category` (3-level tree · 310 rows · seeded from `src/lib/nex-shop/taxonomy.ts` 12 masters + Level 1 + Level 2) | Hierarchical product categorisation · governed enum · parent_id chain | 310 rows across 3 levels · 12 top-level (parent_id IS NULL) |
| **Subject-matter Domain** | The Domain axis (per R-DOMAIN-01) | *What NEX knows about* | Established: accommodation · food · transport · business + commerce (per brain-layer evidence) |
| **Category axis** | Category taxonomy per Domain (per R-DOMAIN-01) | *Governed sub-classification within a Domain* | Marketplace taxonomy IS Category-shaped |
| **Operational Scope** | Per §7.3.B1 | *What NEX operates/processes/delivers* | Marketplace delivery surface (buy/checkout/payment) is currently disabled per commerce-postgres.ts (Stage 6 payment not yet integrated) |
| **Agent/Runtime Identity** | Per §7.3.B1 · AgentId type | *Which agent performs work* | No commerce agent · no marketplace agent |

**Load-bearing architectural evidence:** commerce is at the Domain tier in the brain layer (`WorldVertical` type includes it as a peer to accommodation/food/transport) · has a dedicated world-adapter (`commerce-postgres.ts`) · has capability-registry entry · has HQ page · has Indonesia module. Only the domain-classifier uses the alias `markets` and has an unmounted adapter (§7.3 audit).

### 16.3 · Cross-substrate seller ↔ business overlap

**Direct evidence of the founder's hairbrush example:**

- `nex.mp_seller` (23,580 rows) — sellers as commercial entities
- `nex.service_business` (3,922 rows) — services businesses as commercial entities
- `nex.business_knowledge` (1,258 rows) — Business Domain knowledge
- `nex.business_lead_directory` (2 rows) — business-directory promotion target
- `nex.accommodation_business` (9,230 rows) — accommodation businesses (also commercial entities in a specific Domain)
- `nex.food_business` (22,757 rows) — food businesses (same)

**A seller IS also a business** (same physical entity · different roles depending on Domain context). This confirms the founder's insight: seller/business/service are cross-referenced via the entity, not merged into a single axis. The Bridge (per ADR-0312) is the mechanism that links commerce_seller ↔ business_lead ↔ service_business ↔ accommodation_business when they refer to the same real-world entity.

### 16.4 · Consequences per candidate

**Option A · Commerce is a genuinely distinct Domain:**

| Consequence | Impact |
|---|---|
| Doctrine · commerce becomes a canonical Domain slug (alongside accommodation · food · transport · business per §7.3-B1 evidence) | ✅ Aligns with existing brain-layer commerce vertical · dedicated adapter · capability registry entry · Indonesia commerce module |
| Doctrine · marketplace 310 mp_category values become the Category axis WITHIN commerce Domain (per R-DOMAIN-01 · Category is per-Domain governed classification) | ✅ Natural fit — the 3-level marketplace taxonomy is Category-shaped |
| Doctrine · sellers are commerce entities · linked to Business via Bridge (per ADR-0312) if same entity is both seller AND business-lead | ✅ Consistent with `directory-knowledge.ts` treating 5 verticals in parallel |
| Doctrine · products are commerce-Domain entities · services are business-Domain entities · both are Categories/entities within their respective Domains | ✅ Clarifies the seller/product/service distinction the founder identified |
| Naming alignment · classifier's `markets` alias would need reconciliation to `commerce` OR treated as alias per §7.6 pattern | 🟡 Introduces a §7.6 alias question · not fatal · precedent set by §7.2 (food/food-beverage) |
| Fragmentation risk | LOW · commerce already IS a peer vertical in brain · this ratifies existing architecture |
| Reversibility | HIGH · demotion to Category within Business is a founder-authorised ADR away |
| Substantive risk | ⚠️ Contradicts §7.3.B1 anti-inflation IF Master AI proposes commerce as new invention · but commerce is NOT a new invention · it's already substrate-established across 6+ files as a peer vertical |

**Option B · Marketplace/product taxonomy is orthogonal · does not define an Intelligence Domain:**

| Consequence | Impact |
|---|---|
| Doctrine · marketplace is a delivery surface only · not a knowledge subject | 🔴 Contradicts existing brain-layer commerce vertical (`WorldVertical` type includes `commerce`) · contradicts `commerce-postgres.ts` doctrine header (*"Doctrine parity with accommodation/food/service"*) |
| Doctrine · commerce-postgres.ts adapter would need to be reclassified as non-Intelligence | 🔴 Would break the composer's ability to speak about products (commerce is currently threaded through compare-from-world.ts · goal-tracking.ts · frame-scope-intelligence.ts) |
| Reversibility | LOW · demoting an already-established brain vertical creates code drift |
| Substantive gain | NONE · marketplace product knowledge is legitimately subject-matter (what NEX knows about a phone/laptop/car) |

Not recommended.

**Option C · Category structure within existing Business Domain:**

| Consequence | Impact |
|---|---|
| Doctrine · marketplace becomes a Category-taxonomy within Business Domain (sellers-as-business-lead + commerce-as-subclass) | 🟡 Semantically defensible (sellers ARE businesses) but flattens the seller/product distinction |
| Doctrine · nex.mp_* substrate remains but its records are logically Business-Domain-scoped | 🟡 Would require Business Domain to own commerce_seller + commerce_product AND accommodation_business AND food_business AND service_business — implicitly makes Business a "meta-Domain" that other Domains sit under |
| Cross-Domain query · "hairbrush seller near me" would resolve within Business Domain but need to distinguish product-sellers from service-providers from accommodations | 🔴 Loses the distinction the founder explicitly called out |
| Reversibility | LOW · once Business is meta-Domain, splitting later requires rewiring |
| Substantive gain | Preserves parsimony (one fewer Domain) but at the cost of blurring |

Not recommended.

**Option D · Split model · sellers / products / services treated differently:**

| Consequence | Impact |
|---|---|
| Doctrine · Seller identity is a Business-Domain entity (with `seller_id → business_id` bridge) | ✅ Aligns with §7.3-B1 architectural separation |
| Doctrine · Product is a Commerce-Domain entity (with per-product Category from marketplace taxonomy) | ✅ Preserves commerce brain vertical + adapter + capability |
| Doctrine · Service is a Business-Domain entity OR a distinct Services Domain (deferred to §7.9) | 🟡 §7.9 question · not resolved here |
| Doctrine · marketplace 310 categories = Category axis within Commerce Domain | ✅ Category-shape |
| Doctrine · Router composes across Commerce + Business + Location when queried "hairbrush seller near me" | ✅ Matches founder's example |
| Reversibility | HIGH · if any of the three sub-decisions needs revision, its ADR is separately amendable |
| Substantive gain | Most architecturally honest · but has more surface area than A |

Recommended alongside A. In practice, **Option A already implies Option D** — Option A locks Commerce as a Domain; the seller/product/service distinctions follow naturally from R-DOMAIN-01 anti-substitution and from the substrate shapes (mp_seller/mp_product/service_business are already separate tables).

### 16.5 · Master AI recommendation (labelled · not binding · founder decides)

**Recommendation: Candidate D1 · a hybrid of Option A + explicit Option D framing.**

Grounds:

1. **Commerce IS ALREADY substrate-established as a first-class NEX vertical.** Six independent architectural surfaces (brain vertical enum · commerce-postgres world adapter · capabilities registry · Indonesia commerce module · HQ commerce page · frame-scope-intelligence peer vertical) all treat commerce as a peer to accommodation · food · transport. This is NOT a Master-AI-inferred Domain candidate; it is an already-existing architectural fact.
2. **Anti-Domain-inflation discipline is respected.** Commerce is not being invented from a taxonomy shape (like the ~17 OUTDOOR orphan slugs from §7.4). It is being FORMALLY RECOGNISED — the brain vertical enum already includes it. §7.5 ratifies rather than invents.
3. **Marketplace product taxonomy (310 rows) fits cleanly as Category axis within Commerce Domain** — 3-level hierarchical structure matches Category-shape per R-DOMAIN-01.
4. **The founder's seller/product/service distinction is preserved** by acknowledging Commerce as its own Domain: sellers cross-link to Business Domain via Bridge (ADR-0312) · products are Commerce-Domain entities · services are Business-Domain (or Services-Domain per §7.9).
5. **Reversibility is HIGH** — future founder ADR may demote Commerce to Category within Business if evidence emerges that the peer-vertical treatment was premature.

**Naming reconciliation flagged for §7.6:** the domain-classifier uses `markets` (with unmounted adapter) while the brain vertical enum uses `commerce`. This is a §7.6 alias question — `markets ↔ commerce` may resolve to `commerce` as canonical (matching the more substrate-heavy brain vertical usage) and `markets` as alias, but §7.6 decides.

### 16.6 · Candidate founder decision wordings (founder authorises · amends · or writes their own)

**Candidate D1 (Option A · with explicit seller/product/service framing per founder):**

> *"§7.5 · `commerce` is a canonical NEX subject-matter Domain, ratifying the existing brain-layer commerce vertical (established across `src/lib/nex/brain/frame-scope-intelligence.ts`, `commerce-postgres.ts` world adapter, `capabilities.ts` registry, `src/lib/nex/indonesia/commerce/*` module, `src/app/nex-head-quarters/commerce/page.tsx` HQ surface, and consumer-product noun → commerce mapping in the brain composer). Commerce is a peer Domain to accommodation · food · transport · business, not a subordinate Category. The 310-row `nex.mp_category` taxonomy (sourced from `src/lib/nex-shop/taxonomy.ts` 12 masters + Level 1 + Level 2) is the Category axis within the commerce Domain."*
>
> *"Seller/business identity, product, and service are treated as distinct entities under R-DOMAIN-01 anti-substitution: (a) `nex.mp_seller` rows are commerce entities that MAY cross-link to `nex.business_lead_directory` and other business-facing tables via the Bridge (per ADR-0312) when the same real-world entity operates in multiple Domains; (b) `nex.mp_product` rows are commerce-Domain entities classified by mp_category; (c) `nex.service_business` rows are Business-Domain (or Services-Domain per §7.9) entities distinct from commerce products."*
>
> *"The domain-classifier's `markets` alias (currently with an unmounted adapter) is reconciled via §7.6 · Master AI does not resolve markets↔commerce here."*
>
> *"Marketplace delivery-surface capabilities (buy · add_to_cart · checkout · payment) remain DISABLED per current commerce-postgres.ts doctrine header and per `capabilities.test.ts` (`activated('commerce') === false`); commerce as a Domain refers to KNOWLEDGE of sellers/products/categories, not to a live transactional pipeline. The delivery-surface activation is separate future work."*
>
> *"R-DOMAIN-01 anti-substitution rules apply · Guardian rejects marketplace slugs used in accommodation/food/transport/business Domain fields · rejects Domain slugs used at Category axis · rejects commerce Domain used in Operational Scope or Agent/Runtime Identity axes."*

**Candidate D2 (minimal · Option A only · defers seller/product/service framing):**

> *"§7.5 · `commerce` is a canonical NEX subject-matter Domain, ratifying the existing brain-layer commerce vertical. The 310-row `nex.mp_category` taxonomy is the Category axis within the commerce Domain. Seller/product/service framing and the markets↔commerce alias question are deferred to future §7.x resolutions."*

**Candidate D3 (defer entirely):**

> *"§7.5 · The status of commerce/marketplace as a NEX Domain is deferred pending founder review of the six substrate confluences (brain vertical · adapter · capability · Indonesia module · HQ surface · consumer-product-noun mapping). No canonical Domain locked at §7.5 · no Category axis authored. Founder authorises a follow-up ADR when ready."*

**Master AI recommendation between D1/D2/D3 (labelled · not binding at time of proposal):**

Recommend **D1** because it:
- Ratifies existing architectural fact (not invention)
- Preserves the founder's seller/product/service framing
- Explicitly notes that delivery-surface (buy/checkout) is separately future work
- Respects §7.6 for the markets↔commerce alias question
- Explicitly notes R-DOMAIN-01 anti-substitution
- Reversibility preserved via future founder ADRs

D2 is acceptable if founder prefers minimum doctrine surface.

D3 is acceptable if founder wants to defer commerce Domain recognition until other §7.x items are resolved.

**🔒 FOUNDER DECISION · D1 · LOCKED 2026-09-11**

> *"GREEN LIGHT — I recommend D1. This is the correct place to lock Commerce as a canonical NEX Domain."*
>
> — Philip · 2026-09-11

**Locked canonical wording (verbatim · from founder):**

> *"Decision: D1 · Commerce is a canonical NEX Domain, already substrate-established and peer to the other established NEX Domains. The existing marketplace taxonomy is subordinate to the Domain/Category architecture and does not itself define NEX Domain existence. Seller, Product, and Service remain distinct logical/entity classifications: sellers represent commerce entities/businesses; products represent commerce offerings; services remain separately governed and are not automatically reclassified as commerce products. Cross-Domain relationships shall use the governed Bridge architecture defined by ADR-0312. The existing markets classifier value is not resolved by this decision and remains deferred to §7.6 for alias reconciliation. Commerce knowledge and discovery are distinct from live transactional capability; buy, checkout, add-to-cart, payment, and related transactional activation remain disabled and outside this ADR. R-DOMAIN-01 anti-substitution rules apply. The 310 existing marketplace category values are not yet promoted to canonical Category taxonomy by this decision and require a separate founder-authorised reconciliation/population ADR."*

**Load-bearing architectural basis:**

- Guardian rule: buy/checkout/add-to-cart/payment capabilities remain DISABLED per current `commerce-postgres.ts` doctrine header + `capabilities.test.ts` (`activated('commerce') === false`) · commerce as Domain is KNOWLEDGE, not transaction
- `markets` classifier alias is unresolved · Guardian permits `markets` for classifier purposes pending §7.6 · rejects it as a canonical Domain slug
- No physical row modified · no CHECK constraint added · no code change · no migration · no capabilities activation · no marketplace-taxonomy-promotion

## Section 17 · §7.6 evidence + candidate resolution · 2026-09-11

**Founder directive verbatim 2026-09-11:**

> *"Authorise §7.6 evidence inspection only. Proceed with the next founder decision: §7.6 · Alias Reconciliation. Read-only architectural inspection only. For each pair determine whether the relationship is: true Domain alias/synonym · related but distinct Domains · different architectural axes · operational/runtime naming only · historical/legacy value · unresolved and requiring founder decision. Do not infer equivalence from naming alone. Preserve the five-axis model. Apply the anti-substitution rule."*

Five pairs to reconcile:

1. `markets` ↔ `commerce`
2. `engineering` ↔ `programming`
3. `voice` ↔ `speaking`
4. `transport` ↔ `travel`
5. `rentals` ↔ `accommodation`

Doctrine-only inspection. Preserving §7.1–§7.5 evidence unchanged.

### 17.1 · Pair 1: `markets` ↔ `commerce`

**Evidence · `markets`:**
- domain-classifier `DOMAIN_TRIGGERS.markets` · 8 tokens (`market · markets · pasar · shopping · mall · shop · shops · buy · belanja · toko · supermarket · traditional market · night market · flea market`) · classifier CAN route
- `src/lib/nex/live-chat-completion/adapters/markets-adapter.ts` exists BUT **not mounted** in `nex-conv/chat/route.ts` adapters map (dead path per Domain Brain Coverage Audit §2.7)
- NOT in AgentId · NOT in `nex_lab.rooms` · NOT in NexVertical · NO specialist substrate

**Evidence · `commerce`:** (per §7.5 evidence · already locked)
- Brain `WorldVertical` peer to accommodation/food/transport · dedicated `commerce-postgres.ts` world adapter · `capabilities.ts` entry · Indonesia commerce module · HQ page · consumer-product noun → commerce mapping · 8-table `nex.mp_*` substrate · 23,580 sellers · 310 mp_category · 5 products
- §7.5 D1 lock: commerce is canonical Domain

**Semantic overlap check:**
- markets trigger `night market` — also appears in accommodation activity-taxonomy NIGHTLIFE group · AND in `nex.brain_attractions.attraction_kind` CHECK enum (12 values including `market`)
- markets trigger `mall · shopping` — semantically overlaps with commerce (product-shopping) AND attractions (place-visit) AND business (retail businesses)
- commerce is more specific: sellers · products · offers

**Architectural classification:** ⚠️ **historical/legacy value trending toward alias-of-commerce**. The `markets` classifier value predates the commerce brain-vertical build (Stage 3.34d · Phase 27h · Philip 2026-08-31). Adapter unmounted for months. The 8 trigger tokens overlap Commerce (buy · belanja · toko · supermarket · shopping · mall) and attractions (market · pasar · night market). No specialist infrastructure for `markets` at any tier.

**Conflicts/ambiguities:**
- Chat route dead-path: classifier routes `markets` but no adapter serves the turn
- Semantic scope drift: 3 of 8 tokens (`market · pasar · night market`) are attraction-shaped rather than commerce-shaped
- Legacy timing: markets predates commerce build

**Affected substrates and runtime paths:** classifier + unmounted adapter file only · no substrate · no worker · no capability entry

**Recommended founder decision candidates:**

| Candidate | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **E1-a · commerce canonical · markets full alias · retire dead path** | Reroute all 8 markets triggers to commerce Domain · deprecate markets-adapter.ts · retire the classifier value. Handles 5 clearly-commerce tokens (buy · belanja · toko · supermarket · shopping) cleanly. |
| **E1-b · commerce canonical · markets partial alias · split by token semantics** | Some tokens (buy · toko · supermarket · shopping) resolve to commerce; some (market · pasar · night market · mall) resolve to attractions OR business. Requires per-token routing. |
| **E1-c · keep both as distinct classifier tokens · resolve at future implementation ADR** | Doctrinally treat markets and commerce as different classifier tokens for now · lock canonical Domain = commerce · defer per-token routing. |

**Consequences:**
- E1-a: cleanest architecturally · one canonical Domain (commerce) · classifier value `markets` retired · classifier update required (but no substrate write)
- E1-b: honest to the token-set diversity · but introduces per-token complexity at classifier layer
- E1-c: minimum doctrine surface · defers implementation

**Master AI recommendation (labelled · not binding):** **E1-a** — commerce canonical · markets full alias · retire the dead path. Aligns with §7.5 D1 lock. The three attraction-shaped tokens (`market · pasar · night market`) already have attraction routing available (attractions Domain per §7.3). No substantive loss.

**Confidence:** HIGH · commerce is already substrate-established as Domain per §7.5 lock · markets has zero substrate.

### 17.2 · Pair 2: `engineering` ↔ `programming`

**Evidence · `engineering`:**
- `src/lib/nex/agent-runtime/types.ts:97` — `PositionRuntime.domain: string; // 'engineering' | 'accommodation' | ...` — this is a **free-text description** on the Agent/Runtime Identity axis · example value only · not CHECK-enforced
- Also appears in Supabase `knowledge_records.category` pattern-B rows: `"NEX Trade Knowledge · Design & Engineering"` (18 rows per §7.1 inventory) · `"NEX Trade Knowledge · Design & Engineering · Geometries"` (5 rows) · `"NEX Trade Knowledge · Design & Engineering · Structural Mechanics"` (3 rows) — **trades subject-matter · not code-programming**
- `nex.knowledge_gap` and other tables carry `engineering`-adjacent scope descriptors
- ADR-0021 lists Business Brains (Estimator · Bookkeeper · Marketing · Operations · Sales · Compliance) · engineering NOT among them
- ADR-0017 Trade Brain Contract lists trade brains: Staircase · Electrical · Plumbing · Roofing · Carpentry · Landscaping · Bricklaying · Plastering · Tiling · Painting · Heating · Solar · Kitchen Install · Bathroom Install · etc. — engineering NOT explicitly among them (but Design & Engineering appears in Supabase as a sub-category)

**Evidence · `programming`:**
- NO direct `programming` slug in domain-classifier · AgentId · NexVertical · nex_lab.rooms · category-registry
- BUT `code` IS in domain-classifier (§7.3) · `programmer` IS an AgentId · code-intent-registry has 10 code intents · code Domain locked per §7.3
- Founder ADR-0308 explicitly frames code as a NEX1 Domain: "code is a domain of NEX (programming role)"

**Semantic scope:**
- **engineering** in agent-runtime is a role/discipline descriptor · in Supabase is a trades-subject-matter (staircase engineering · design engineering · structural engineering)
- **programming** is a specific subset of engineering (software engineering discipline) · maps to code Domain per §7.3
- These are DIFFERENT subject-matters at multiple levels

**Architectural classification:** ⚠️ **different architectural axes AND different subject-matters** — NOT aliases.

Two distinct usages of `engineering`:
1. In `agent-runtime.PositionRuntime.domain` free-text: describes what an agent does (role · Agent/Runtime Identity-adjacent)
2. In Supabase `knowledge_records.category` "Design & Engineering": trades subject-matter (structural · design · staircase engineering)

`programming` doesn't overlap either usage cleanly. Programming → code Domain (§7.3 · established) is a distinct subject-matter.

**Conflicts/ambiguities:**
- `engineering` in agent-runtime is on Agent/Runtime Identity axis (5th axis per §7.3-B1)
- `engineering` in Supabase pattern-B is subject-matter (trades Domain candidate)
- `programming` is on Domain axis via `code` (§7.3 · locked)
- These occupy THREE different logical positions

**Affected substrates and runtime paths:**
- `agent-runtime` PositionRuntime.domain free-text · runtime metadata
- Supabase knowledge_records ~26 rows across Design & Engineering hierarchical variants
- Code Domain: nex.concepts · code-intent-registry · programmer AgentId · nex-agent module · programmer worker (RUNNING per §7.3 audit · PID 29476)

**Recommended founder decision candidates:**

| Candidate | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **E2-a · not aliases · different axes** | `engineering` is NOT the same as `programming`. `engineering` in agent-runtime is Agent/Runtime Identity role. `engineering` in Supabase is trades subject-matter. `programming` is code Domain per §7.3. Three distinct positions. Founder-authorised split. |
| **E2-b · engineering = candidate trades subject-matter Domain (deferred)** | Recognise `engineering` in Supabase as a trades-adjacent Domain candidate · defer to §7.10 or a dedicated ADR alongside staircase/kitchen/flooring reconciliation. Programming remains as code Domain per §7.3. |
| **E2-c · reject engineering as a Domain · treat agent-runtime usage as legacy free-text · treat Supabase usage as sub-Category** | Engineering-as-agent-runtime-domain is legacy free-text · rename to a role field. Engineering-in-Supabase is a sub-Category within a broader trades Domain (staircase · door · etc.) · deferred pending trade Domain reconciliation. Programming stays as code Domain. |

**Consequences:**
- E2-a: honest architectural split · records the multi-axis usage clearly · founder decides later how to reconcile trades subject-matter
- E2-b: creates a candidate trades Domain (Engineering) that would need substrate evidence to promote — flag for §7.10
- E2-c: cleanest cleanup · but touches agent-runtime free-text renaming · code impact

**Master AI recommendation (labelled · not binding):** **E2-a** — not aliases · different axes · founder-authorised split. Aligns with §7.3-B1 five-axis discipline. Engineering-as-role (agent-runtime) stays on the 5th axis. Engineering-as-trades-subject (Supabase) is a distinct §7.10 candidate. Programming remains code Domain (§7.3).

**Confidence:** HIGH · substrate evidence clearly separates the three usages.

- travel: adapter + worker only · zero substrate · classifier-routed to legacy fallback

- E4-b: architectural simplification but loses semantic clarity · code impact
- E4-c: preserves both concepts but changes travel's tier · Category taxonomy authoring
- E4-d: honest about substrate asymmetry · gives travel time to develop

**Master AI recommendation (labelled · not binding):** **E4-a** — transport and travel are distinct Domains · both remain locked · substrate asymmetry acknowledged. Aligns with the founder's caution: *"transport ↔ travel should not be casually treated as an alias until Master AI establishes whether they represent the same subject or different concepts."* Evidence shows different subjects.

**Confidence:** MEDIUM-HIGH · semantic distinction clear · substrate asymmetry does not imply equivalence.

- Comment in category-registry line 133: `"Rentals categories (Task #90+)"` — future work reference

**Evidence · `accommodation`:**

- Full specialist substrate: 9,230 accommodation_business rows + 46,306 provenance + adapter + worker
- 9 accommodation category-registry entries (hotel · villa · guesthouse · homestay · resort · hostel · apartment · kos + broad accommodation)

**Semantic scope:**
- rentals: things you rent — could be short-term equipment (bikes · scooters · cars) OR short-term accommodation (Airbnb-style)
- accommodation: places to stay (any duration)
- Overlap: short-term accommodation-rentals (villa rental · homestay rental) — but these already exist as accommodation Categories (villa · homestay)
- Non-overlap: bike/scooter/car rentals · equipment rentals

**Architectural classification:** ⚠️ **unresolved · likely a split concept requiring founder decision**.

Two distinct interpretations of `rentals` exist in NEX:
1. **Accommodation-adjacent rentals** (short-term accommodation stays) → these already have Category coverage in accommodation Domain (villa · homestay · apartment · resort)
2. **Equipment/vehicle rentals** (bike · scooter · car · gear · tools) → no dedicated Domain · partial substrate (nex.bike_rental_listing)

The Lab pairing with `activities` ("Activities & Rentals Lab" · one lab · one schema `nex_lab_activities`) suggests rentals is treated as an activity-adjacent concept (rent-a-bike-for-your-holiday) rather than an accommodation-adjacent concept. But the NexVertical enum treats rentals as a PEER vertical to accommodation and food.

**Conflicts/ambiguities:**
- NexVertical says rentals is peer to accommodation (distinct)
- Lab pairing says rentals is bundled with activities (subordinate to activities)
- Substrate (bike_rental_listing) says rentals is equipment-shaped (not accommodation-shaped)
- No domain-classifier trigger · no worker · no adapter · no brain table
- The overlap with commerce is worth flagging: renting a bike is a commercial transaction · commerce covers sellers/products/offers — should a rental be a commerce offering type?

**Affected substrates and runtime paths:**
- NexVertical enum (would need amendment if rentals demoted)
- nex_lab_activities Lab schema (would need renaming if rentals split from activities)
- nex.bike_rental_listing + nex.bike_model tables (partial equipment-rental substrate)
- category-registry rentals-child entries deferred to Task #90+

Decompose rentals via applicable_domains (per §7.4 C1 pattern) OR via per-Domain Category. Demote from NexVertical. |
| **E5-b · rentals is a distinct Domain (equipment-rental scope) · separate from accommodation** | Preserve NexVertical peer status. `rentals` covers equipment/vehicle/gear rentals only. Requires substrate build (worker · adapter · brain table · Lab room split from activities). Short-term-accommodation-rentals remain in accommodation Domain. |
| **E5-c · unresolved · deferred to future rentals-scope ADR** | Do not resolve at §7.6. Keep NexVertical entry as-is. Category-registry rentals-child entries remain deferred. Founder resolves when a rentals use case emerges (Task #90+). |
| **E5-d · rentals is a Category-shaped concept within Commerce Domain (§7.5 D1)** | Following §7.5 D1: rentals could be a commerce offering variant (mp_product_condition-adjacent · or a new offering_type). Alternative: rentals is applicable to Commerce Domain as a per-slug applicable_domains[] annotation on activity-taxonomy slugs. |

**Consequences:**
- E5-a: cleanest per R-DOMAIN-01 anti-substitution · decomposes the concept into its natural homes · requires updating NexVertical (code change)
- E5-b: preserves NexVertical peer status · requires substantial substrate build · anti-inflation risk (creates a new Domain from thin evidence)
- E5-c: least commitment · defers ambiguity · risks contaminating downstream work
- E5-d: rentals-as-commerce-offering-type is architecturally interesting but ties rentals to commerce · loses the accommodation-adjacency

**Master AI recommendation (labelled · not binding):** **E5-a** — rentals is NOT a distinct Domain · it is a Category-tier or offering-type concept across multiple Domains (accommodation for short-stay rentals · commerce for equipment/vehicle rentals · activities for tours-and-rentals bundling). Aligns with anti-Domain-inflation discipline: rentals substrate is minimal (9 bike rentals + 50 bike models · zero classifier · zero worker · zero adapter · zero Lab room of its own).

However E5-c (defer) is acceptable if founder wants more evidence before decomposing NexVertical.

**Confidence:** MEDIUM · substrate evidence weakly supports rentals-as-non-Domain · but NexVertical peer status is a real architectural artefact.

## Section 18 · §7.6.5 evidence + candidate resolution · 2026-09-11

**Founder directive verbatim 2026-09-11:**

> *"Authorise §7.6.5 evidence presentation only. Proceed to the next founder decision: rentals ↔ accommodation. Reconcile the existing evidence in §17.5 and inspect any additional load-bearing evidence necessary to determine whether rentals should be: a canonical Domain · a Category-tier concept applicable across existing Domains · an Accommodation Category for short-stay rentals while equipment rentals receive another governed home · a related-but-distinct Domain · or remain unresolved pending further evidence."*

**Founder's key reframing question:**

> *"Does 'rental' describe a subject-matter Domain, or does it describe a type of commercial/service activity that can occur across Domains?"*

Evidence preserved from §17.5. This section adds new load-bearing evidence and reframes the analysis.

### 18.1 · New evidence beyond §17.5

**18.1.1 · `nex.bike_rental_listing` schema shape (verified via live read-only column probe):**

```
rental_id · slug · name · city · neighbourhood · whatsapp_e164 · photo_url
preferred_bike_slug · preferred_categories (ARRAY)
helmets_included · raincoats_included
hotel_villa_dropoff · tank_full_on_rental · airport_pickup_on_arrival
price_per_day_idr · price_per_week_idr · price_per_month_idr
has_buy_option · buy_price_idr
status · rating_avg · rating_count · created_at · updated_at
```

**Interpretive facts from this schema:**

- **`has_buy_option` + `buy_price_idr`** — the SAME listing supports both RENT and BUY offerings on the same physical bike. This is direct architectural evidence that "rent" is an OFFERING TYPE alongside "buy" on a commerce entity · NOT a distinct subject.
- **`hotel_villa_dropoff` boolean** — bike rental cross-references Accommodation Domain (hotels · villas) as a service feature.
- **`airport_pickup_on_arrival` boolean** — bike rental cross-references Transport Domain (airport) as a service feature.
- **`price_per_day / week / month`** — offering-time-window · a rental-specific attribute pattern applicable to any offering that can be time-metered (bike-rent · car-rent · scooter-rent · vacation-rental accommodation · equipment-rent).
- **`preferred_bike_slug` + `preferred_categories`** — pointers to `nex.bike_model` (50 rows) · bike-specific product catalogue.

**Verified live substrate:**
- `nex.bike_rental_listing`: **9 rows** across 9 different Indonesian cities (Malang · Lombok · Jakarta · Ubud · Sanur · Canggu · Yogyakarta · Bandung · Gili Trawangan) — one seed per city
- `nex.bike_model`: **50 rows** (bike product catalogue)

**18.1.2 · Broader provider/service architecture (migrations 130–138):**

The bike_rental_listing is part of a broader provider/service/driver architecture:

- `130_nex_bike_model_and_driver.sql` — bike model + driver tables
- `131_nex_driver_extras.sql` — driver extras
- `132_nex_bike_rental_listing.sql` — bike rental substrate
- `133_nex_rental_airport_pickup.sql` — airport pickup as a rental feature
- `134_nex_provider_and_service_request.sql` — provider + service request substrate
- `138_driver_profile_compat_view.sql` — driver profile view

Plus:
- `090_nex_driver_network.sql` — driver network foundation
- `094_nex_parcel_lifecycle.sql` — parcel delivery (transport-adjacent)

**Interpretive fact:** NEX has a **provider · service · driver · rental** architecture that spans Transport (drivers · airport pickup) + Commerce (offerings including rent-vs-buy) + Accommodation (villa dropoff) + Business (providers · service requests) Domains. Rentals is one commercial offering type within that broader provider/service architecture.

**18.1.3 · Accommodation taxonomy explicit rental cross-references:**

From `src/lib/nex/intelligence-storage-grid/accommodation/taxonomy.ts`:

| Line | Slug | Note referencing rental |
|---|---|---|
| 79 | `holiday-home` | alias includes `"vacation rental"` |
| 187 | `caravan` | requires_evidence_note: *"verify commercial rental vs private ownership · static-caravan common in UK holiday parks"* |
| 188 | `mobile-home` | requires_evidence_note: *"verify commercial rental vs long-term residence"* |
| 189 | `campervan-accommodation` | requires_evidence_note: *"usually vehicle-based · verify pitch/site vs vehicle rental"* |
| 196 | `group-accommodation` | alias includes `"whole-property rental"` |

**Interpretive fact:** Accommodation taxonomy already treats "rental" as an alias/mode indicator INSIDE accommodation Categories · not as a distinct Domain. Short-stay rentals are already served by accommodation Category slugs (`holiday-home` · `villa` · `apartment` · `homestay` · etc.).

**Accommodation with short-stay rental-shaped categories** (verified via live query): `nex.accommodation_business` where category IN (`villa`, `apartment`, `homestay`) = **176 rows**. These are all rental-shaped accommodation listings already living inside accommodation Domain.

**Also line 590 of activity-taxonomy.ts** — `child_facilities: readonly string[]` example includes `"stroller_rental"` — treating rental as a facility attribute of an accommodation, not a Domain.

**18.1.4 · No classifier / no worker / no adapter / no Lab room / no ADR calls rentals a Domain:**

- `rentals` NEVER appears in `src/lib/nex/brain/*` code (Grep returned zero matches)
- `rentals` NEVER appears as a classifier trigger token
- `rentals` NEVER appears as an `AgentId` value
- `rentals` NEVER appears as an ADR-authored subject-matter Domain
- `rentals` only appears at 3 places in the whole doctrine surface: (1) `NexVertical` enum · (2) Lab room display name pairing with activities · (3) category-registry as deferred future work (Task #90+)

**18.1.5 · Category-registry deferred rentals sub-categories:**

From `src/lib/nex/category-registry.ts:33-34`: *"Future: add Food categories here (Phase B follow-up · migrate wheel from its inline food entries) and Rentals categories (Task #90+)."*

Line 272-277: *"FOOD (Phase B follow-up · seed entries · registry migration deferred until the existing DirectoryPanel.tsx wheel is refactored to read from here) — Deliberately EMPTY here for Phase A."* — same pattern applies to rentals.

**Interpretive fact:** Rentals categories were EXPLICITLY DEFERRED to future task at the time of category-registry authoring (2026-08-22 · Task #89 Phase A). The NexVertical `rentals` entry was created as a placeholder without concrete sub-categories — deferred to future substrate emergence.

### 18.2 · The reframing under founder's key question

**Founder's key question restated:**

> *"Does 'rental' describe a subject-matter Domain, or does it describe a type of commercial/service activity that can occur across Domains?"*

**Evidence-based answer (architectural · not linguistic):**

The substrate treats "rental" as a **commercial offering type** at multiple observable places:

1. **Same-entity dual offering** — `nex.bike_rental_listing.has_buy_option + buy_price_idr` — one entity supports both rent AND buy modes on the same physical bike. The bike is the subject; rent/buy is the offering shape.
2. **Cross-Domain feature attributes** — bike rentals reference Accommodation (hotel_villa_dropoff) AND Transport (airport_pickup_on_arrival) as service features · rental is applied ACROSS Domain contexts.
3. **Accommodation short-stay rentals** — already served by accommodation Category slugs (villa · apartment · homestay · holiday-home) with rental treated as alias/mode.
4. **No Domain-tier substrate** — no classifier trigger · no worker · no adapter · no brain-vertical membership · no ADR authored as subject-matter Domain.
5. **Time-window pricing pattern** (per-day / per-week / per-month) is a rental-shape offering attribute applicable to any offering that can be time-metered.

**Conclusion:** The architectural evidence supports the interpretation that "rental" describes a **type of commercial/service activity** that can occur across multiple Domains · NOT a subject-matter Domain of its own. This aligns directly with the founder's reframing question.

### 18.3 · Consequence tables per candidate

**Candidate E5-a · Rentals is a Category-tier / offering-type concept across multiple Domains:**

| Consequence | Impact |
|---|---|
| Doctrine · rentals is NOT a canonical Domain · it is a cross-cutting classification | ✅ Aligns with 5 substrate confluences (dual-offering listing · cross-Domain features · accommodation aliases · no Domain-tier substrate · time-window pricing) |
| Doctrine · short-stay accommodation rentals continue to live in Accommodation Domain via existing Categories (villa · apartment · homestay · holiday-home) | ✅ No accommodation substrate touched · aligns with `accommodation/taxonomy.ts` which already treats rental as alias |
| Doctrine · equipment/vehicle rentals (bike · scooter · car · gear) live in Commerce Domain as offering-type distinct from `mp_product_condition` (new/used/refurbished) — rent-vs-buy is a distinct offering dimension | ✅ Aligns with §7.5 D1 · Commerce Domain covers sellers/products/offers |
| Doctrine · rental-with-service (rent-a-driver · rent-with-airport-pickup) lives via cross-Domain feature attributes on the entity (as bike_rental_listing does today with hotel_villa_dropoff + airport_pickup_on_arrival) | ✅ Preserves the observed multi-Domain reference pattern |
| Doctrine · `rentals` in NexVertical is deprecated pending updated vertical registration in a future implementation ADR | 🟡 Requires code change to remove/rename NexVertical entry · flagged for future implementation ADR |
| Doctrine · `nex.bike_rental_listing` substrate remains untouched · rentals-as-offering-shape does not require its own specialist substrate | ✅ Zero migration |
| Fragmentation risk | LOW · rentals is decomposed into its natural homes across Domains |
| Reversibility | HIGH · future founder ADR may promote rentals to Domain if independent architectural evidence emerges |

**Candidate E5-b · Rentals is a distinct Domain (equipment-rental scope):**

| Consequence | Impact |
|---|---|
| Doctrine · rentals is a peer Domain to accommodation/food/transport/business/travel/attractions/code/commerce | 🔴 CONTRADICTS anti-Domain-inflation discipline · rentals lacks 6-substrate-confluence evidence (has NexVertical + Lab pairing only · no classifier + no worker + no adapter + no brain-vertical + no ADR) |
| Doctrine · substrate build required · rentals-adapter · rentals-worker · rentals-classifier · nex.brain_rentals table | 🔴 Substantial substrate authoring · anti-Domain-inflation violation (creating Domain from thin evidence) |
| Fragmentation risk | 🔴 HIGH · creates a Domain from taxonomy-shape reasoning · same class of error that §7.3/§7.4 anti-inflation discipline prevents |
| Reversibility | LOW · once a Domain is locked · demotion requires substrate rewiring |

**Not recommended.**

**Candidate E5-c · Defer entirely:**

| Consequence | Impact |
|---|---|
| Doctrine · rentals status remains UNRESOLVED · founder revisits when more evidence emerges | 🟡 Preserves the one-at-a-time discipline for genuinely ambiguous cases |
| NexVertical `rentals` remains as-is · bike_rental_listing remains as-is | 🟡 No substrate change |
| Blocking risk for downstream work | 🟡 Constitutional layer cannot complete until rentals resolves · but §7.7-§7.10 can proceed if founder authorises |
| Reversibility | HIGH · defer is trivially reversible |

**Candidate E5-d · Rentals is a Commerce-offering-type:**

| Consequence | Impact |
|---|---|
| Doctrine · rentals is a specific offering-type value within Commerce Domain (alongside `mp_product_condition` enum but on a different dimension: rent-vs-buy) | 🟡 Architecturally clean for equipment-rental · misses short-stay accommodation rentals |
| Doctrine · `nex.mp_product.offering_type` field candidate (`rent \| buy \| rent-with-buy-option`) · not yet defined | 🟡 Requires Commerce Domain schema evolution · future ADR |
| Cross-Domain applicability | 🔴 Would need to be replicated in Accommodation Domain OR handled via Bridge (ADR-0312) — creates fragmentation |
| Fragmentation risk | 🟡 Medium · offering-type is commerce-only in this candidate · short-stay rentals don't get the same treatment |

**E5-d is essentially a subset of E5-a** — same treatment for equipment rentals but doesn't explicitly cover short-stay accommodation rentals.

### 18.4 · Master AI recommendation (labelled · not binding)

**Recommendation: E5-a · rentals is a Category-tier / offering-type concept across multiple Domains.**

Grounds:

1. **Substrate treats rentals as offering-type at multiple observation points** — `has_buy_option` on same rental listing · time-window pricing · cross-Domain feature attributes (hotel_villa_dropoff · airport_pickup_on_arrival)
2. **Accommodation short-stay rentals are already served** by accommodation Categories (villa · apartment · homestay · holiday-home · caravan · mobile-home · campervan-accommodation · group-accommodation) — no gap
3. **Equipment rentals fit Commerce Domain cleanly** (per §7.5 D1) — rent-vs-buy is an offering shape · sellers can offer rentals as one commerce mode
4. **Anti-Domain-inflation discipline is honoured** — rentals has ONE substrate (bike_rental_listing 9 rows · thin evidence) · not enough for Domain-tier promotion
5. **The founder's reframing question answered honestly**: "rental" describes a type of commercial/service activity that can occur across Domains · not a subject-matter Domain
6. **Reversibility preserved** — if equipment-rental data grows substantially and independent architectural evidence emerges (classifier + worker + adapter + brain-vertical + Lab room), a future founder ADR can promote Rentals to Domain

**Secondary recommendation:** if E5-a is locked, an implementation ADR (candidate slot ADR-0314a.2.x · NexVertical Rentals Deprecation) authors the code change to remove `rentals` from `NexVertical` enum · founder-authorised · separate from doctrine.

**E5-c (defer) is acceptable** if founder wants more evidence before decomposing. But the evidence collected here is substantial enough to answer the founder's key question in the negative (rentals is not a subject-matter Domain).

**Not recommended:** E5-b (creates Domain from thin evidence · anti-inflation violation) · E5-d (commerce-only misses accommodation short-stay rentals).

### 18.5 · Candidate founder decision wordings

**Candidate G1 (E5-a · comprehensive · answers founder's reframing question):**

> *"§7.6.5 · rentals is NOT a canonical NEX Domain. ### 18.7 · Founder clarification 2026-09-11 · rentals as broad cross-Domain classification/activity

**Founder directive verbatim 2026-09-11 (post initial §18.1-§18.6 evidence presentation):**

> *"Yes — that changes the §7.6.5 decision materially. What you're describing is not 'Accommodation rental.' It is a broad Rental/Leasing subject category covering physical assets such as: 🚗 Cars · 🏍️ Motorbikes · 📷 Cameras · 💻 Laptops · 🎸 Music equipment · 🛠️ Tools/equipment · 🎥 Video equipment · 🚲 Bicycles · ⛺ Outdoor equipment · and potentially many more. That actually supports keeping rentals distinct from accommodation."*
>
> *"Do NOT make rentals a Category under Accommodation. Instead, the architectural model should be: Rental = commercial/service activity involving an asset · while the asset itself belongs to its relevant subject Domain."*
>
> *"So I would not create a 'Rentals Brain.' Instead: Domain → Category → Entity/Asset → commercial/service activity · with Rental potentially being a governed cross-Domain Category/activity classification."*
>
> *"Founder clarification for §7.6.5: rentals is intended to cover rental of assets across multiple subject areas, including cars, motorbikes, cameras, laptops, music equipment and other rentable equipment/assets. Therefore do not evaluate rentals solely through the existing accommodation/short-stay implementation. Reconcile rentals as a potentially cross-Domain classification/activity, with the underlying rented asset remaining associated with its appropriate Domain. Do not create a Rentals Brain. Do not automatically demote rentals to Accommodation. Present the architectural consequences and founder decision candidates, then STOP. Gate 3 remains CLOSED and the freeze holds."*

**Founder-provided architectural model (verbatim illustrative table):**

| Rental listing | Asset subject | Rental classification |
|---|---|---|
| Motorbike rental | Transport | Rental |
| Car rental | Transport | Rental |
| Camera rental | Commerce / Products | Rental |
| Laptop rental | Commerce / Products | Rental |
| Music equipment rental | Commerce / Products | Rental |
| Tool rental | Commerce / Products / Trades | Rental |
| Bicycle rental | Transport | Rental |
| Apartment short-stay | Accommodation | Rental/short-stay category |

**Founder's summarising statement:**

> *"Accommodation can have short-stay rentals, but Accommodation does not own the concept of rental. Rental needs to work across the whole Indonesian ecosystem you're building."*

### 18.8 · Revised architectural model (following founder clarification)

**Correction to §18.1-§18.6 interpretation:** the initial G1 recommendation framed rentals as decomposing primarily into Accommodation + Commerce homes. This was insufficiently broad. Per founder clarification, rentals is a **cross-Domain classification/activity** whose scope spans:

- **Transport** — cars · motorbikes · scooters · bicycles (vehicle rentals for transport)
- **Commerce / Products** — cameras · laptops · music equipment · video equipment (product rentals as commercial offering shape)
- **Business / Trades / Services** — tools · construction equipment · industrial gear (business rentals · trade equipment · service-provider rentals)
- **Accommodation** — short-stay apartments · villas · homestays · vacation rentals (only when the asset is lodging-shaped)
- **Attractions / Activities** (per §7.4) — outdoor equipment · sports gear · tour bundling · surfboards · kayaks (activity-adjacent rental as one manifestation)

**Reframed architectural model (per founder's verbatim):**

```
Domain (per axis 2 · subject-matter of the ASSET)
  ↓
Category (per axis 3 · governed classification of the asset within its Domain)
  ↓
Entity/Asset (the physical object · e.g. a specific bike, camera, apartment)
  ↓
Commercial/Service Activity (rent · buy · lease · borrow · subscribe · service-hire)
```

- **Rental is the activity applied to the asset**, not the asset's identity
- **The asset's Domain remains authoritative** for what it IS
- **Rental is a governed cross-Domain classification/activity value** (per axis choice below)

**Axis membership question · founder decides:**

Under R-DOMAIN-01's five-axis architecture, "rental" as a cross-Domain activity classification could live at either of:

- **Axis 3 (Category) · cross-Domain** — following the §7.4 C1 precedent (Shared cross-Domain Activity Category with per-slug `applicable_domains[]`)
- **A new sub-axis or attribute · offering-type / activity-type** — a distinct classification dimension for "what commercial activity is being performed with this asset" (rent · buy · lease · service · etc.)

Both preserve rentals-as-not-a-Domain. Both keep the asset's Domain as authoritative. The choice between them affects Guardian schema and future taxonomy authoring surface.
