**The constitutional rule established by this lock (per founder's summarising statement):**

> **Activity ≠ Domain.**

**Applies to the following activity concepts** (illustrative · not exhaustive · not locked here · flagged for future §7.x / ADR authoring under the same rule):

- booking (reservations across accommodation/food/transport/attractions/activities)
- delivery (parcel/food/goods across Transport + Commerce + Food)
- subscription (recurring commerce · service-subscription · membership)
- installation (trades service · furniture assembly · equipment fitting)
- repair (device/vehicle/property repair across relevant Domains)
- maintenance (recurring service on assets)
- hire (short-term human labour engagement · adjacent to rental)
- rental (LOCKED here as canonical case)
- resale (used-goods secondary commerce)
- fitting (garments · dental · optical · custom services)
- cleaning (property/vehicle/laundry service)
- consultation (professional advice · legal/medical/business)

Each is an ACTIVITY applied to an ASSET or SUBJECT in its own Domain. None becomes a Domain of its own without independent architectural evidence per feedback_no_domain_inflation_from_orphan_slugs.md.

**Load-bearing architectural basis:**

- `nex.bike_rental_listing.has_buy_option + buy_price_idr` — same entity supports rent AND buy modes · direct evidence that rental is offering-type not identity
- `nex.bike_rental_listing.hotel_villa_dropoff + airport_pickup_on_arrival` — cross-Domain feature attributes proving rental crosses Accommodation + Transport at attribute-tier
- Accommodation taxonomy already treats rental as alias/mode (holiday-home · caravan · mobile-home · campervan-accommodation · group-accommodation with vacation-rental / whole-property-rental aliases)
- No classifier · no worker · no adapter · no ADR-authored subject-matter Domain for rentals
- Founder's illustrative table (Motorbike→Transport · Camera→Commerce · Tool→Commerce/Trades · Apartment→Accommodation) directly maps to the R-DOMAIN-01 anti-substitution model

**Enforcement implications (doctrine · not implemented by this ADR):**

- Guardian rule: use of `rentals` as a canonical Domain-typed value is a G-2 non-bypass violation per R-DOMAIN-01
- Guardian rule: rental-activity classification (once authored in ADR-0314a.2.y) is legitimate at whichever axis founder authorises (Category cross-Domain per §7.4 C1 pattern OR new activity-type sub-axis)
- Guardian rule: asset's Domain remains authoritative — a bike rental record has `domain = "transport"` (bike as transport asset) and `activity = "rental"` (not `domain = "rentals"`)
- No physical row modified · no CHECK constraint added · no code change · no NexVertical amendment (deferred to ADR-0314a.2.x) · no rental-activity taxonomy populated (deferred to ADR-0314a.2.y) · no Guardian rule installed · no capabilities activation

**Companion ADRs generated or required by this decision:**

- **ADR-0314a.2.x · NexVertical Rentals Deprecation** — future founder-authorised implementation ADR · removes/renames the `rentals` NexVertical entry after ADR-0314a.2.y locks the replacement classification pattern
- **ADR-0314a.2.y · Rental Cross-Domain Classification Registry** — future founder-authorised doctrine ADR · defines: (a) canonical rental-activity vocabulary; (b) per-value `applicable_domains[]` (per §7.4 C1 pattern) OR alternative activity-type sub-axis; (c) governance rules; (d) fail-closed policy; (e) anti-substitution enforcement — this is the ADR that decides whether rental sits at axis-3 (Category cross-Domain) or at a new activity-type/offering-type sub-axis
- **Future ADR (candidate) · Cross-Domain Activity/Offering Classification Framework** — generalises the Activity ≠ Domain rule to booking · delivery · subscription · installation · repair · maintenance · hire · resale · fitting · cleaning · consultation · founder-authorised · deferred until the concrete need for such generalisation emerges

**Reversibility path preserved:** a future founder-authorised ADR MAY promote a specific rental-scope (e.g. commercial-vehicle-rental) to Domain IF independent architectural evidence emerges (classifier + worker + adapter + brain-vertical + substantial substrate + demand signal per anti-Domain-inflation discipline).

**Constitutional layer state after §7.6.5 lock:**

Domain axis (8 members · unchanged from §7.5): accommodation · food · transport · business · travel · attractions · code · commerce.

Rentals is **explicitly not** in the Domain axis. It joins the Activity/Offering classification pattern (subject to ADR-0314a.2.y).

## Section 19 · §7.7 evidence + candidate resolution · 2026-09-11

**Founder directive verbatim 2026-09-11:**

> *"Authorise §7.7 evidence presentation only. Proceed to §7.7 · other sentinel — canonical or fail-closed? Perform read-only architectural reconciliation of every relevant occurrence of `other` across: Domain values · Category values · classifiers · schemas and CHECK constraints · Labs · crawlers · adapters/workers · Router · Chat · NEX1 · marketplace taxonomy · existing NEX data · ADRs and doctrine. Determine whether `other` is: a legitimate canonical classification · a temporary/operational fallback · an unknown/unresolved state that must fail closed · valid only at particular taxonomy axes · or requires different treatment depending on axis. Do not assume other has the same meaning across the five axes."*

**Founder-provided guidance:**

> *"This one is worth doing carefully because other is particularly dangerous in an autonomous system: if treated as a real classification everywhere, it can become a dumping ground that hides knowledge gaps. If treated as unknown everywhere, it may destroy legitimate governed categories where an explicit catch-all is actually useful. So the correct answer may well be axis-specific, rather than simply 'canonical' or 'fail-closed.'"*

Doctrine-only inspection. Preserving §7.1–§7.6.5 evidence unchanged.

### 19.2 · Semantic distinction: `other` vs `unknown` (load-bearing)

These are **different values with different meanings** across NEX substrates:

| Concept | Meaning | Founder-authored? | Legitimate result of classification? |
|---|---|---|---|
| `other` | *"The classifier ran and determined the value does NOT fit any named category. It is intentionally in the catch-all bucket."* | ✅ Yes (when authored as part of a taxonomy) | ✅ Yes · row is CLASSIFIED |
| `unknown` | *"The classifier could not determine a value. The value is unresolved."* | ✅ Yes (as a sentinel) | 🟡 Yes but the row is NOT-YET-classified · pending resolution |
| Fail-closed reject | *"The write is refused because the value doesn't conform to the enum."* | Behavioural (Guardian rule) | ❌ No · row is rejected · never persisted with that value |

**Substrate evidence for the distinction:**

- Domain classifier returns `"unknown"` when no triggers match (line 171) OR when ambiguous (line 181) — **classifier does not silently produce `other`**
- `nex.brain_attractions.attraction_kind = 'other'` is populated by the executor's `attractionKind()` fallback — this is a DIFFERENT act (Guardian-permitting `other` at Category axis · row persists · classified as catch-all)
- These are two architecturally different mechanisms

**Conflating `other` with `unknown` (or with fail-closed reject) would collapse three distinct semantic states into one · which is exactly what the founder warned against.**

### 19.3 · Axis-by-axis analysis

Per founder's own hint: *"the correct answer may well be axis-specific."*

**Axis 1 · LAM Object Type (14 canonical types + Bridge · per ADR-0309.1):**
- LAM Object Type is architectural identity (Language · Semantic · People-Say · Domain Knowledge · Provenance · Governance · TE-verdict · Contradiction · Confidence · Version · Runtime · Competency · Authorisation · Bridge)
- If a row doesn't fit any of 14, NEX cannot govern it (Guardian schema shape · authorisation policies · verifier semantics all depend on LAM Object Type)
- **`other` is NOT LEGITIMATE at this axis.** Fail-closed to reject.
- No substrate observation of `other` at LAM Object Type axis (correctly absent).

**Axis 2 · Domain (8 canonical Domains locked per §7.5):**
- Domain is subject-matter (accommodation · food · transport · business · travel · attractions · code · commerce)
- Classifier already uses `"unknown"` as its named sentinel when no Domain fits (not `other`)
- **`other` is NOT LEGITIMATE at Domain axis.** Fail-closed to `unknown` at classifier boundary; Guardian rejects at Domain-typed field writes.
- Rationale: subject-matter is either a governed Domain or it's unknown; there's no meaningful "the subject is other" case.

**Axis 3 · Category (per-Domain governed classification · per R-DOMAIN-01):**
- This is where the observed `other` values live
- **`other` MAY be legitimate at Category axis WHEN AND ONLY WHEN** the parent taxonomy explicitly authors it as a governed catch-all (per §7.4 C1 activity taxonomy pattern where `OTHER` is one of 11 groups with the intent-note *"catch-all · MUST become a specific group over time"*)
- **Anti-dumping-ground guardrail required:** the 27/32 (84%) attraction_kind=other observation is EXACTLY the dumping-ground failure the founder warned against — if `other` becomes >50% of a Category-taxonomy's usage, the taxonomy is undersized and Guardian must flag it for founder review
- **NOT permitted at Category axis for un-authored taxonomies:** Guardian rejects `other` writes if the parent taxonomy has not explicitly declared `other` as a governed value

**Axis 4 · Operational Scope (5 known values per §7.3-B1 · canonical values pending ADR-0314a.2.t):**
- Operational Scope covers image · news · voice · chat · monetization (per §7.3-B1)
- **`other` is NOT LEGITIMATE at this axis.** Operational scope is either a registered scope or fail-closed to reject/unknown_operational_scope.
- Rationale: operational scopes are the runtime areas of NEX; there's no "other operational area" outside registered scopes; unknown scopes indicate a system architecture gap requiring founder authoring.

**Axis 5 · Agent/Runtime Identity (11 AgentIds per §7.3-B1 · canonical values pending ADR-0314a.2.u):**
- AgentId is registered agents (programmer · accommodation · master_ai · speaking · vision · travel · business · food · construction · healthcare · transport)
- **`other` is NOT LEGITIMATE at this axis.** Agent identity is either a registered agent or fail-closed to reject.
- Rationale: agents must be explicitly registered with runtime discipline (heartbeat · PID · founder-stop per §7.3 audit) — no anonymous "other" agent can exist.

### 19.4 · Anti-dumping-ground evidence

**The observed dumping-ground pattern (84% attraction_kind=other) validates the founder's concern.**

Two forces contribute:

1. **Undersized Category taxonomy.** `nex.brain_attractions.attraction_kind` has only 12 CHECK-permitted values (temple · beach · waterfall · mountain · national_park · museum · monument · historical_site · viewpoint · market · village · other). Real attraction data harvested from OSM/tourism_boards likely includes cultural venues · religious sites (mosque · church · shrine) · parks · gardens · beaches · viewpoints etc. that don't cleanly fit the 11 named values. The `activities-executor.ts` `attractionKind()` function has explicit keyword rules for only 9 named values before falling through to `"other"`.
2. **Executor fallback code.** `activities-executor.ts:46 · return "other";` — the executor DEFAULTS to `other` rather than skipping the row or requiring named classification.

**Founder-flagged risk realised:** *"if treated as a real classification everywhere, it can become a dumping ground that hides knowledge gaps."* The 84% observation is exactly this failure mode.

**Mitigation options (not authored in this ADR · flagged for future implementation ADRs):**

- **Anti-dumping threshold**: Guardian emits a warning event if `other` exceeds N% (e.g. 30%) of a Category taxonomy's row count · founder reviews and authors Category-taxonomy expansion
- **Executor policy**: executors avoid persisting `other` when the source data has richer signals (e.g. OSM `tourism` · `historic` · `leisure` tags that could resolve to a more-specific named Category if the taxonomy included the values)
- **Category taxonomy expansion**: founder-authored expansion of attraction_kind CHECK enum to reduce `other` share (e.g. adding shrine · park · garden · scenic_road · lookout · religious_site · cultural_venue etc.)

### 19.5 · Consequences per candidate

**Candidate H1 · Axis-specific: `other` is legitimate only at Category axis when explicitly authored · fail-closed at other axes · with anti-dumping-ground guardrail:**

| Consequence | Impact |
|---|---|
| Doctrine · axis-specific `other` semantics | ✅ Matches founder's own framing hint |
| Doctrine · LAM Object Type / Domain / Operational Scope / Agent Identity: `other` REJECTED (fail-closed to `unknown` or Guardian reject) | ✅ Aligns with fail-closed defaults across R-01/R-05/R-07/R-11/R-12/R-13 |
| Doctrine · Category axis: `other` PERMITTED only when parent taxonomy explicitly authors it as governed value | ✅ Preserves legitimate governed catch-all use cases (activity taxonomy OTHER · nex.brain_attractions attraction_kind other) |
| Doctrine · Anti-dumping-ground guardrail: Guardian emits warning when `other` share exceeds a threshold (percentage · founder-authored) | ✅ Directly addresses the observed 84% dumping-ground failure |
| Doctrine · `other` and `unknown` are DIFFERENT values with different meanings · MUST NOT be conflated | ✅ Preserves the semantic distinction (classified-as-catch-all vs unresolved) |
| Doctrine · classifier fail-closed sentinels use `unknown` (Domain axis · already correct in domain-classifier.ts) | ✅ Aligns with existing code · no change required |
| Substrate impact | None immediate. Future implementation ADR authors the Guardian threshold rule + Category-taxonomy expansion for attraction_kind (84% dumping-ground fix). |
| Reversibility | HIGH · axis-specific rule is compositional · specific axes can amend independently |

**Candidate H2 · Fail-closed everywhere · `other` is not a canonical value at any axis:**

| Consequence | Impact |
|---|---|
| Doctrine · every axis rejects `other` · Guardian is uniform | 🟡 Uniformity has appeal · simpler doctrine |
| Doctrine · `other` in current substrate (27 brain_attractions rows · 2 business_lead_directory rows) becomes legacy · must be reclassified or Guardian-blocked | 🔴 Destroys 27 already-persisted attraction rows that carry legitimate positional data (lat/lng · name) · violates ADR-0035 Two Laws precedent (classify never reject) |
| Doctrine · governed catch-all Categories (activity taxonomy OTHER · marketplace 'Other') lose the ability to serve genuine catch-all cases | 🔴 Loses architectural flexibility · forces excessive Category taxonomy expansion |
| Fragmentation risk | 🔴 HIGH · destroys legitimate substrate |

**Candidate H3 · Canonical `other` at all axes · permissive:**

| Consequence | Impact |
|---|---|
| Doctrine · every axis permits `other` as a legitimate value | 🔴 Domain axis would carry `other` (classifier could produce `other` alongside `unknown` · double-sentinel · confusion) |
| Doctrine · LAM Object Type could be `other` | 🔴 Breaks the entire authorisation policy framework (R-10) which depends on knowing LAM Object Type |
| Fragmentation risk | 🔴 VERY HIGH · dumping-ground failure becomes system-wide |

Not recommended · directly contradicts fail-closed doctrine of R-01/R-05/R-07/R-11/R-12/R-13.

**Candidate H4 · Defer entirely:**

- Preserves the ambiguity · risks contaminating downstream constitutional decisions and implementation
- Not recommended given the observed 84% dumping-ground pattern is a live substrate condition

### 19.6 · Master AI recommendation (labelled · not binding)

**Recommendation: Candidate H1 · axis-specific.**

Grounds:

1. **Directly matches the founder's framing hint** (*"the correct answer may well be axis-specific"*)
2. **Preserves legitimate governed catch-all use cases** at Category axis (activity taxonomy OTHER group · marketplace category `other` · attraction_kind catch-all when properly bounded)
3. **Aligns with fail-closed doctrine** across R-01/R-05/R-07/R-11/R-12/R-13 for the other axes
4. **Preserves the semantic distinction** between `other` (classified-catch-all) and `unknown` (unresolved) that the domain-classifier already correctly implements
5. **Includes the anti-dumping-ground guardrail** that directly addresses the observed 84% failure at brain_attractions
6. **Consistent with ADR-0035 Two Laws** (Classify Never Reject) for image knowledge · governance-taxonomy fail-closed is a distinct architectural concern from content classification
7. **Reversibility HIGH** · axis-specific rule composes cleanly with future amendments

**Not recommended:** H2 (destroys legitimate substrate · violates classify-never-reject for content) · H3 (dumping-ground failure system-wide · breaks R-10 authorisation policy framework) · H4 (defers a live substrate condition).

### 19.7 · Candidate founder decision wordings

**Candidate H1-comprehensive:**

> *"§7.7 · The `other` sentinel is treated axis-specifically:*
>
> *(1) At Axis 1 (LAM Object Type · 14 canonical types) · Axis 2 (Domain · 8 canonical) · Axis 4 (Operational Scope · pending ADR-0314a.2.t) · Axis 5 (Agent/Runtime Identity · pending ADR-0314a.2.u): `other` is NOT a legitimate value. Fail-closed. A row that does not resolve to a registered value at these axes is classified as `unknown` at classifier boundary (as domain-classifier.ts already does at lines 171 · 181) and Guardian rejects a write with an unregistered value.*
>
> *(2) At Axis 3 (Category · per-Domain governed classification): `other` MAY be a legitimate canonical catch-all Category value ONLY WHEN the parent Category taxonomy explicitly authors it as a governed value in a founder-authorised taxonomy ADR. Guardian rejects a write with `other` in a Category-typed field if the parent taxonomy has not declared `other` as governed.*
>
> *(3) Anti-dumping-ground guardrail: when a governed Category taxonomy has `other` as an authorised value, Guardian emits a warning event when the share of rows carrying `other` exceeds a threshold to be defined in a future implementation ADR (candidate slot ADR-0314a.2.z · Anti-Dumping-Ground Guardrail). The observed 84% (27/32) `attraction_kind = 'other'` share at `nex.brain_attractions` is flagged as the load-bearing evidence of this failure mode and is explicitly identified as requiring Category-taxonomy expansion in a future founder-authorised ADR.*
>
> *(4) Semantic separation: `other` and `unknown` are DISTINCT values with different meanings. `other` means 'classified-as-catch-all' (the row is classified). `unknown` means 'not-yet-resolved' (the row is not classified). Guardian rules and Truth Engine verdicts MUST treat them separately. Conflation of the two is a G-2 non-bypass violation.*
>
> *(5) Cross-reference with ADR-0035 Two Laws (Classify Never Reject): the image-knowledge classification-band model applies to content extraction. Governance-taxonomy fail-closed is a distinct concern. Both doctrines remain in force. No change to image bands or to Guardian governance rules by this decision.*
>
> *(6) The existing legacy rows (`nex.brain_attractions.attraction_kind = 'other'` 27 rows · `nex.business_lead_directory.category_group = 'other'` 2 rows) remain unchanged. Category taxonomy expansion and Guardian rule authoring are separate future founder-authorised implementation ADRs."*

**Candidate H1-minimal:**

> *"§7.7 · `other` is axis-specific: legitimate as a governed canonical catch-all at Category axis when explicitly authored in the parent taxonomy · fail-closed (Guardian rejects · classifier uses `unknown`) at LAM Object Type · Domain · Operational Scope · Agent/Runtime Identity axes. `other` and `unknown` are distinct values (classified-catch-all vs unresolved). Anti-dumping-ground threshold enforcement deferred to future implementation ADR. Existing legacy `other` rows preserved unchanged."*

**Master AI recommendation between H1-comprehensive · H1-minimal · H2 · H3 · H4:**

Recommend **H1-comprehensive** because it explicitly:
- Names the observed 84% dumping-ground failure (making it a matter of record · not silent)
- Codifies the `other` vs `unknown` semantic distinction (which the code already correctly implements but doctrine has not yet stated)
- Preserves ADR-0035 Two Laws precedent for image knowledge
- Identifies the future implementation slot (ADR-0314a.2.z · Anti-Dumping-Ground Guardrail)
- Preserves reversibility

H1-minimal is acceptable if founder prefers minimum doctrine surface · but the observed dumping-ground failure benefits from explicit mention.

>

**Decision provenance footer:**

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Decision** | H1-comprehensive · axis-specific `other` · LAM Object Type + Domain + Operational Scope + Agent/Runtime Identity: `other` PROHIBITED (fail-closed to `unknown` where applicable · Guardian rejects at write) · Category axis: `other` CONDITIONALLY PERMITTED only when parent taxonomy explicitly authors it as governed bounded catch-all · `other` and `unknown` constitutionally DISTINCT · silent conversion between them forbidden · existing historical `other` rows preserved unchanged · anti-dumping-ground governance authored in future ADR-0314a.2.z · ADR-0035 Two Laws remains scoped to image-knowledge |
| **Decided by** | Philip |

| **Effective from** | Other-Sentinel-Governance.v1.0.0 |
| **Supersedes** | none |
| **Reason** | Live substrate carries 27/32 (84%) `nex.brain_attractions.attraction_kind = 'other'` — direct evidence of the dumping-ground pattern that hides knowledge gaps. Axis-specific rule preserves the observed legitimate use case (governed catch-all at Category axis) while preventing `other` from becoming a universal escape hatch that erodes classification integrity. The constitutional `other ≠ unknown` distinction ensures NEX can grow without hiding gaps: unresolved classifications flow through the `unknown` state which drives research/acquisition/classification; genuinely-catch-all Categories flow through explicitly-authored bounded `other` values. Existing historical rows preserved to honour prior classification decisions; future taxonomy expansion authored via separate implementation ADRs. |

**Constitutional rule established by this lock:**

> **`other` ≠ `unknown`. Silent conversion between the two is forbidden.**
>
> - `other` = the classifier ran and determined the value falls into a governed bounded catch-all Category authored by founder
> - `unknown` = the classifier could not resolve the value · classification is not yet determined
> - `unknown` must trigger knowledge-gap workflow (research · acquisition · re-classification) · NOT be silently converted to `other`
> - `other` must NOT be used at LAM Object Type · Domain · Operational Scope · or Agent/Runtime Identity axes

**Load-bearing architectural basis:**

- Live substrate evidence: `nex.brain_attractions.attraction_kind = 'other'` = 27/32 rows (84%) — dumping-ground failure directly observed and named as smoking gun
- Domain classifier already correctly uses `"unknown"` (not `other`) at fail-closed decision points (lines 171 · 181 of domain-classifier.ts) — the code precedent supports the constitutional distinction
- Fail-closed doctrine across R-01 / R-05 / R-07 / R-11 / R-12 / R-13 for governance taxonomy already establishes the reject-unregistered-values pattern
- ADR-0035 Two Laws (Classify Never Reject · IMMUTABLE · 2026-07-27) precedent applies to IMAGE knowledge — governance-taxonomy fail-closed is a distinct concern; both doctrines remain in force at their respective scopes

**Enforcement implications (doctrine · not implemented by this ADR):**

- Guardian rule: any write of `other` at LAM Object Type · Domain · Operational Scope · or Agent/Runtime Identity axis is a G-2 non-bypass violation
- Guardian rule: `other` at Category axis is permitted ONLY when the parent Category taxonomy explicitly authors it as a governed value in a founder-authorised taxonomy ADR
- Guardian rule: silent conversion of `unknown` to `other` (or vice versa) at any axis is a G-2 non-bypass violation
- Classifier boundary: fail-closed to `unknown` at Domain axis (already correct in domain-classifier.ts)
- Anti-dumping-ground rule (future ADR-0314a.2.z): Guardian emits warning event when `other` share exceeds a founder-authored threshold on any Category taxonomy; the 27/32 attraction_kind failure is flagged as evidence prompting the rule
- No physical row modified · no CHECK constraint added · no code change · no classifier change · no executor change · no capabilities activated · existing legacy `other` rows preserved unchanged

**Companion ADRs generated or required by this decision:**

- **ADR-0314a.2.z · Anti-Dumping-Ground Governance for Category-Level `other`** — future founder-authorised implementation ADR · defines (a) which Category taxonomies may author `other` as a governed bounded catch-all · (b) Guardian warning threshold when `other` share exceeds N% of a Category's row count · (c) Category-taxonomy expansion procedure when threshold breached · (d) reconciliation policy for legacy `other` rows (e.g. the 27 `nex.brain_attractions` rows)
- **Future ADR (candidate slot) · Category Taxonomy Expansion for `nex.brain_attractions.attraction_kind`** — founder-authored expansion to reduce the 84% `other` share by adding named values (shrine · park · garden · scenic_road · religious_site · cultural_venue · etc.) or by re-mapping legacy rows

**Cross-references (constitutional layer state after §7.7 lock):**

Locked constitutional rules established across the layer so far:
- R-DOMAIN-01 · three-axis anti-substitution
- §7.3-B1 · five-axis architecture (LAM Object Type · Domain · Category · Operational Scope · Agent/Runtime Identity)
- §7.6.5 G4 · Activity ≠ Domain (activities are cross-Domain classifications · assets stay in their Domains)
- **§7.7 H1-comprehensive · `other` ≠ `unknown` · axis-specific catch-all** (this lock)

Domain axis membership unchanged (8 Domains from §7.5): accommodation · food · transport · business · travel · attractions · code · commerce.

## Section 20 · §7.8 evidence + candidate resolution · 2026-09-11

**Founder directive verbatim 2026-09-11:**

> *"Authorise §7.8 evidence presentation only. Proceed to §7.8 · Supabase `NEX ` prefix reconciliation. Perform read-only architectural inspection of every relevant occurrence of the `NEX ` prefix across Supabase knowledge_records and all related fields, code, classifiers, Router mappings, domain/category taxonomies, provenance, titles, categories, subcategories, and downstream consumers. Determine whether `NEX ` is: logical taxonomy data, a physical/display naming convention, provenance/ownership metadata, historical legacy formatting, or a mixture requiring field-specific treatment."*

**Founder-provided warning (load-bearing):**

> *"One thing I would watch closely: don't let Master AI assume that because `NEX ` looks cosmetic, it is safe to strip. The audit should establish whether it has become embedded in category identity, routing, queries, provenance, or external references."*

Doctrine-only inspection. Preserving §7.1–§7.7 evidence unchanged.

### 20.1 · Complete field-level inventory of `"NEX "` prefix in Supabase knowledge_records (live read-only probe 2026-09-11)

Total rows: **3,627**

| Field | Rows with `"NEX "` prefix | Share | Interpretation |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| `category` | **3,627** | **100%** | Universal prefix · every category value in the substrate carries it (per §7.1 evidence) |
| `canonical_owner` | **3,627** | **100%** | Universal prefix · every ownership record carries it — NEW FINDING · 61 distinct canonical_owner values (see §20.2 below) |
| `subcategory` | **0** | **0%** | Prefix NEVER used in subcategory · these are free-text descriptions (e.g. *"Vertical infill members between newel posts..."*) |
| `title` | **15** | **0.4%** | Almost never · edge cases like `"NEX Business Brain"` · `"NEX Digital Identity"` · most titles are natural (`"Newel Posts"` · `"American Black Walnut"` · `"Stair Handrails"`) |
| `authored_by` | **0** | **0%** | Prefix NEVER used · uses author identity (`"Research Claude"` · `worker:knowledge-extractor@...`) |

**Interpretive fact:** the `"NEX "` prefix appears in **exactly two** knowledge_records fields at 100% coverage: `category` and `canonical_owner`. It is absent from `subcategory`, `authored_by`, and nearly absent from `title`. This is not an accidental convention · it is a systematic pattern.

### 20.2 · `canonical_owner` distinct-value pattern reveals a code-embedded prefix bug

**Top-20 distinct `canonical_owner` values (verified via live probe · 61 total distinct values):**

| Rows | canonical_owner |
|---:|---|
| 403 | `"NEX NEX door · Knowledge Extractor"` |
| 247 | `"NEX NEX kitchen · Knowledge Extractor"` |
| 200 | `"NEX NEX flooring · Knowledge Extractor"` |
| 16 | `"NEX NEX Components · Knowledge Extractor"` |
| 12 | `"NEX NEX Trade Knowledge · Knowledge Extractor"` |
| 11 | `"NEX NEX staircase · Knowledge Extractor"` |
| 8 | `"NEX NEX Trade Knowledge · Components · Staircase Components · Knowledge Extractor"` |
| 7 | `"NEX NEX Trade Knowledge · Design & Engineering · Knowledge Extractor"` |
| 7 | `"NEX NEX Trade Knowledge · Historical Context · Knowledge Extractor"` |
| 5 | `"NEX Product · Trade Knowledge team · Materials specialty"` |
| 5 | `"NEX NEX Trade Knowledge · Design & Engineering · Geometries · Knowledge Extractor"` |
| 5 | `"NEX NEX Components · Staircase · Structural Joinery · Knowledge Extractor"` |
| 4 | `"NEX Product · Customer Guidance team"` |
| 4 | `"NEX NEX Platform Architecture · Knowledge Management · Knowledge Extractor"` |
| 3 | `"NEX Staircase · Components sub-cluster"` |
| 3 | `"NEX NEX Customer Guidance · Decision Support · Knowledge Extractor"` |
| 3 | `"NEX NEX Trade Knowledge · Design & Engineering · Structural Mechanics · Knowledge Extractor"` |
| 3 | `"NEX NEX Trade Knowledge · Design · Ergonomics & Spatial Design · Knowledge Extractor"` |
| 3 | `"NEX NEX Components · Handrail Specifications · Knowledge Extractor"` |
| 2 | `"NEX Product · Trade Knowledge team · Staircase Components specialty"` |

**Two distinct patterns emerge:**

**Pattern P1 · auto-extracted (861+ rows · dominant):** `"NEX NEX <category> · Knowledge Extractor"` — includes `"NEX NEX door"` · `"NEX NEX kitchen"` · `"NEX NEX flooring"` etc. **This is a DOUBLE-NEX bug** — the "NEX " prefix appears twice.

**Pattern P2 · human-authored (dozens of rows):** `"NEX <Team> · <Sub-team>"` — includes `"NEX Product · Trade Knowledge team · Materials specialty"` · `"NEX Staircase · Components sub-cluster"` · `"NEX Product · Customer Guidance team"`. Single "NEX " prefix followed by org-scope naming.

### 20.3 · Root cause of Pattern P1 double-NEX (code-embedded)

**Load-bearing finding:** `src/lib/nex/brain/workers/knowledge-extractor.ts:446`

```
canonical_owner: `NEX ${rec.category} · Knowledge Extractor`,
```

The knowledge-extractor worker **naively concatenates** the string `"NEX "` with the value in `rec.category`. But `rec.category` ALREADY carries the `"NEX "` prefix (per §7.1: 100% of category values start with `"NEX "`). Result: `"NEX " + "NEX door" + " · Knowledge Extractor"` = `"NEX NEX door · Knowledge Extractor"`.

This is the SINGLE runtime code reference to the `"NEX "` prefix in NEX Intelligence code (I searched `src/lib/nex/brain/*` and `src/lib/nex/live-chat-completion/*` and other locations · no other code depends on the prefix).

**Line 119 of the same file** mentions `"NEX Knowledge Extractor"` in the system prompt (LLM identity naming · operational · not substrate-dependent).

**Interpretation:**

- The `"NEX "` prefix in `category` has become **embedded in exactly one runtime code path**: knowledge-extractor.ts:446 concatenation
- That code path produces a **bug** (double-NEX) in 861+ rows
- No classifier · Router · Truth Engine · Guardian · adapter · worker · Lab · CHECK constraint · index · query filter · downstream consumer depends on the `"NEX "` prefix in `category`
- **Stripping `"NEX "` from `category` would BENEFICIALLY FIX** the double-NEX bug at knowledge-extractor.ts:446 (the concatenation would produce correct `"NEX door · Knowledge Extractor"` instead of `"NEX NEX door · Knowledge Extractor"`)

### 20.4 · Semantic vs presentation usage analysis

Applying Rule 15 (physical representation must NOT silently define logical authority · per ADR-0310):

| Field | Physical value | Logical meaning | Rule 15 status |
|---|---|---|---|
| `category` | `"NEX door"` (100%) | Category slug within trades scope · maps to some future canonical Category taxonomy (per §7.4/§7.5 style · not yet locked for Trade Knowledge · deferred to future ADR) | Physical value carries prefix · logical value SHOULD NOT · Rule 15 REQUIRES separation |
| `canonical_owner` | `"NEX NEX door · Knowledge Extractor"` OR `"NEX Product · Trade Knowledge team"` | Ownership metadata identifying the NEX-scope organisational team or worker origin | The `"NEX "` in canonical_owner is an ORG-SCOPE MARKER · legitimate metadata (distinguishes NEX-authored ownership from external contributors) · but the double-NEX bug is a BUG |
| `subcategory` | Free-text description | Free-text description | No prefix · no mapping needed |
| `title` | Natural language name | Natural language name | Prefix rare (15 rows / 0.4%) · likely accidental · edge case |
| `authored_by` | Author identity | Author identity | No prefix · separate concern |

### 20.5 · Downstream dependencies audit

Searched `src/**/*.ts`:

- **domain-classifier.ts** — does NOT reference `"NEX "` prefix (uses its own DOMAIN_TRIGGERS + `"unknown"` fail-closed) — independent

- **NEX1 / nex-agent module** — does NOT reference `"NEX "` prefix — independent
- **Hammerex Supabase tables** — separate product · out of NEX Intelligence scope

### 20.6 · Risks of removal vs preservation

**Risks of removing the `"NEX "` prefix from `category`:**

| Risk | Severity | Mitigation |
|---|---|---|
| 3,627 rows require migration | Medium — measurable · reversible | Founder-authorised implementation ADR authors the migration |
| knowledge-extractor.ts:446 would produce different string (correct-NEX instead of double-NEX) | Low — this is the current bug being fixed | Fix or accept in the migration ADR |
| Historical audit trail (`canonical_owner` refers back to `"NEX door"` etc. in old rows) | Low — canonical_owner is metadata · not identity | Preserve legacy `canonical_owner` values as-is · re-derive future ones from stripped category |
| External integrations expecting `"NEX door"` etc. | Very Low — no external product observed to consume this | Verify at implementation time |
| Retrieval / search queries relying on `"NEX "` prefix | Low — no such queries observed in code | Verify at implementation time |

**Risks of preserving the `"NEX "` prefix:**

| Risk | Severity | Mitigation |
|---|---|---|
| Double-NEX bug remains active | Medium — 861+ rows of dirty ownership metadata | Fix knowledge-extractor.ts:446 to detect existing prefix |
| Physical value continues to violate Rule 15 (prefix embedded in logical-Category-adjacent field) | Medium — Rule 15 already handles this by treating physical as non-authoritative | Doctrine already permits · but future implementation must translate at Router boundary |
| Future canonical Category taxonomy authoring inherits confusion (should ADR-0314a.2.o author `"NEX door"` or `"door"` as canonical?) | High if unresolved before §7.4 Category-authoring companions | RESOLVE at §7.8 · this ADR |
| Cross-substrate values compared as strings (Supabase `"NEX door"` vs nex_dev `staircase` per Category or Domain axis) | Medium — inconsistency friction | Address via Bridge / mapping layer in future ADR |
| External systems ever consuming this substrate would inherit the prefix | Low — no observed external consumer | Verify at implementation time |

### 21.2 · Cross-reference finding · service has stronger brain-vertical evidence than business

Under §7.5 D1 lock pattern (commerce ratified as Domain because it appeared as brain-vertical peer + specialist substrate + adapter + composer + capability + Indonesia module):

**`service` has 5 of 6 substrate confluences** matching the §7.5 D1 pattern:
- ✅ Brain WorldVertical peer (frame-scope-intelligence.ts)
- ✅ Dedicated world adapter (`service-postgres.ts`)
- ✅ Specialist substrate (`nex.service_business` 3,922 rows)
- ✅ Composer/capability/entity-attribute wiring
- ✅ Full provider ecosystem (7 adjacent tables)
- ❌ NOT in domain-classifier · NOT AgentId · NOT NexVertical · NOT Lab room

**Contrast — `business` (per §7.3 lock) has 3-way concurrence** but at different tiers:
- ✅ domain-classifier
- ✅ AgentId (worker registered)
- ✅ nex_lab.rooms seed
- ❌ NOT a brain WorldVertical peer (business absent from frame-scope-intelligence.ts:350 vertical set)
- ❌ No dedicated `business-postgres.ts` adapter
- 🟡 Business substrate is thin (business_lead_directory 2 rows · business_knowledge 1,258 rows populated only with accommodation+food)

**Interpretation:** `business` and `service` occupy DIFFERENT architectural positions:
- `business` = coarse classifier/routing tier + Lab-harvest scope (broadly commercial entities · "shop hours" · "phone number" · "opening hours" style questions applicable to any business)
- `service` = brain-vertical + specialist substrate tier (professional/personal service businesses: pharmacies · salons · dentists · gyms · opticians · car-repair)

These are NOT aliases · NOT the same architectural layer. Both may legitimately be Domains at different tiers.

### 21.3 · Semantic scope of `service` (per live substrate)

3,922 real Indonesian businesses across 6 specialist categories · all `status=listed · verified=false · claimed=false`:

- **Health-adjacent (2,409 · 61%):** pharmacies · dentists · opticians (candidates for Healthcare Domain per §7.10 · AgentId `healthcare` exists)
- **Personal-service (615 · 16%):** salons (hair · beauty · nail)
- **Vehicle-service (606 · 15%):** car-repair
- **Wellness (292 · 7%):** gyms

The substrate treats these as one Service Domain. Some subsets (pharmacies · clinics · dentists) may later split to Healthcare Domain per §7.10.

Grounds:

3. **Business and Services occupy DIFFERENT architectural positions** — business at classifier/routing tier · service at brain-vertical/specialist-substrate tier — not aliases
4. **Anti-inflation discipline honoured** — Services is ratified from existing architectural fact · not invented
5. **Domain axis grows to 9 members** (adds `services` alongside the 8 locked from §7.5)
6. **Reversibility preserved** — future ADRs may promote Healthcare subset (pharmacies/dentists/clinics) into distinct Healthcare Domain per §7.10 · Services boundary can adjust

### 21.6 · Candidate founder decision wordings

This ADR does not lock that migration."*

That's exactly the scalable architecture you wanted."*

- Guardian rule: `services` is a canonical Domain slug at Axis 2 (Domain) — use in Domain-typed fields is permitted
- Guardian rule: use of `services` at LAM Object Type · Category · Operational Scope · Agent/Runtime Identity axes is a G-2 non-bypass violation per R-DOMAIN-01
- Guardian rule: sub-service Categories (Dentist · Hairdresser · Salon · Car Repair · Gym · Optician · Massage · etc.) live inside Services Domain · authored in future founder-authorised Services Category-taxonomy ADR · NOT as separate Domains
- Guardian rule: Services Domain and Business Domain are peer Domains at different architectural tiers · no substitution or aliasing
- Guardian rule: business_lead_directory continues to route rows to specialist Domain tables (accommodation_business · food_business · service_business) via business-lead-executor · preserves cross-Domain catch-all pattern
- Anti-inflation rule: no Healthcare / Dentist / Hairdresser / Massage / etc. Brain shall be created merely because sub-service data exists — Domain promotion requires independent architectural evidence per feedback_no_domain_inflation_from_orphan_slugs.md
- No physical row modified · no CHECK constraint added · no code change · no adapter/worker/classifier/Lab change · no Guardian rule installed by this decision

## Section 22 · §7.10 evidence + candidate resolution · 2026-09-11

**Founder directive verbatim 2026-09-11:**

> *"Authorise §7.10 evidence presentation only. Tell Master AI to examine every candidate, not just Healthcare and Engineering, and explicitly classify each as: KEEP AS DOMAIN / CATEGORY / ACTIVITY / OPERATIONAL SCOPE / EXISTING DOMAIN / DEFER. Then STOP."*

**Founder-provided decision framework (verbatim):**

> *"Does this have enough independent architectural evidence to deserve its own Domain, or can it cleanly live inside an existing Domain? That prevents the system becoming: Dentist Brain · Massage Brain · Hotel Brain · Product Brain · Timber Brain · Door Brain · Kitchen Brain..."*

Doctrine-only inspection. Preserving §7.1–§7.9 evidence unchanged.

### 22.1 · Compact classification matrix (10 candidates)

Applying the §7.5 D1 / §7.9 L1 substrate-confluence test (6-source: brain WorldVertical · dedicated adapter · specialist substrate · composer wiring · provider/companion ecosystem · classifier/AgentId/Lab/NexVertical membership):

| Candidate | Substrate confluences | Master AI classification |
|---|:---:|---|
| **Healthcare** | 1 of 6 (AgentId only) | 🟡 **CATEGORY within Services Domain** (existing §7.9 L1) · promotion to Domain deferred pending independent architectural evidence |
| **Engineering** | 0 of 6 (Supabase legacy pattern-B + agent-runtime free-text role) | 🟡 **DEFER** — fragmentary usage across multiple axes · not enough for Domain promotion · not enough for demotion · founder decides |
| **Cultural** | 0 of 6 (only in activity-taxonomy CULTURE group) | 🔵 **CATEGORY within Attractions Domain** (existing §7.3) |
| **History** | 0 of 6 (brain_did_you_know_indonesia 39 · Supabase Historical Context 9) | 🔵 **CATEGORY within Attractions Domain** + **cross-Domain ACTIVITY** (historical-context attribute per §7.6.5 G4 pattern) |
| **Places** | 4 of 6 (brain WorldVertical peer · composer wiring · recommendation-intent target · orchestrate handling) — **BUT already ALIASED to `attractions` at recommendation-intent.ts:123** | 🟠 **ALIAS of Attractions Domain** (per §7.6.1 markets/commerce pattern) — canonical = `attractions` · `places` is the brain-vertical form · deferred implementation ADR reconciles the naming |
| **Locations** | 0-1 of 6 (`nex.brain_location` 0 rows · aliased with Places in recommendation-intent.ts:64 as place-synonym cluster) | 🟢 **CROSS-DOMAIN METADATA** — every Domain entity has a location (city · district · neighbourhood coordinates already in accommodation_business/food_business/service_business etc.) · NOT a subject-matter Domain · fits the founder's country-expansion note *"country/local context should be another governed dimension of the knowledge rather than another NEX brain"* |
| **Products** | Existing Domain (Commerce §7.5 D1) | ✅ **EXISTING DOMAIN · Commerce** — products are commerce entities (nex.mp_product) per §7.5 D1 lock |
| **Marketing** | Split (§7.3 mixed case) | ↔ Operational marketing = **OPERATIONAL SCOPE** (§7.3 B1 lock preserved) · subject-matter Marketing candidate = **DEFER** (still no independent architectural evidence emerged since §7.3) |
| **Hotels** | Existing Category (per category-registry `parentVertical: "accommodation"`) | ✅ **CATEGORY within Accommodation Domain** (existing) — 523 discovered rows already routed via `businessTable=nex.accommodation_business · categoryFilter=category='hotel'` |
| **Trade Knowledge** (staircase · door · kitchen · flooring · bathroom · timber · tools · lighting · roofing · interior · marketing per ADR-0033 image-brains) | 1 of 6 (Supabase legacy pattern-A/B substrate 3,627 rows · ADR-0033 image-brain isolation which is a DIFFERENT tier) | 🟡 **DEFER** — pending Supabase Migration ADR-0314a.2.aa · founder may later authorise a Trades meta-Domain (candidate ADR) with staircase/door/kitchen etc. as Categories inside it · matches CLAUDE.md UK-trades positioning |

### 22.2 · Per-candidate detail

**22.2.1 · Healthcare · CATEGORY within Services Domain (existing §7.9 L1)**

Evidence:
- ✅ AgentId `healthcare` registered (worker-healthcare.ts) — 1 confluence
- ❌ No specialist substrate (no `nex.healthcare_business` · no brain_healthcare)
- ❌ No brain-vertical peer status
- ❌ No dedicated adapter
- ❌ Not classifier · not Lab · not NexVertical
- 🟡 **2,409 healthcare-adjacent rows LIVING IN nex.service_business** (pharmacies 1,907 · dentists 326 · opticians 176)

Interpretation: Healthcare has 1 of 6 substrate confluences · well below the §7.5 D1 threshold. Existing content (pharmacies · dentists · opticians · potential clinics/hospitals) already lives in Services Domain. Per §7.9 L1 sub-taxonomy pattern *"Dentist · Hairdresser · Salon · Car Repair · Gym · Optician · Massage · etc. live INSIDE Services Domain as Categories"* — Healthcare-adjacent categories join that pattern.

Master AI recommendation: **CATEGORY within Services Domain** (existing §7.9 L1). Future Healthcare Domain promotion possible ONLY if independent architectural evidence emerges: specialist substrate authored · brain-vertical peer status · dedicated adapter · classifier target · demand signal.

**22.2.2 · Engineering · DEFER**

Evidence:
- ❌ Not AgentId · not classifier · not brain vertical · no specialist substrate · no adapter · not Lab
- 🟡 26+ Supabase pattern-B rows carrying `"NEX Trade Knowledge · Design & Engineering"` (legacy subject-matter · pending §7.8 J1 physical→logical mapping)
- 🟡 In `agent-runtime.PositionRuntime.domain` free-text as example value `'engineering'` (per §7.6.2 · Agent/Runtime Identity role descriptor · not subject-matter)

Interpretation: Engineering occupies 3 distinct logical positions (per §7.6.2 E2-a analysis): agent-runtime role · Supabase trades subject-matter · not-a-Domain-slug-anywhere. Fragmentary usage across multiple axes · insufficient for Domain promotion OR clean demotion.

Master AI recommendation: **DEFER**. Not enough evidence to lock either direction. Founder may authorise a Trades meta-Domain later (which would house Engineering as Category) OR authorise Engineering-as-Category-within-a-broader-scope · but this decision needs the Trade Knowledge question resolved first.

**22.2.3 · Cultural · CATEGORY within Attractions Domain (existing §7.3)**

Evidence:
- ❌ Not AgentId · not classifier · not brain vertical · no specialist substrate · no adapter · not Lab
- 🟡 CULTURE group in activity-taxonomy (12 slugs: temple · mosque · church · palace · museum · historic_site · traditional_village · cultural_performance · festival · market · craft_centre · heritage_area) — per §7.4 C1 cross-Domain Category taxonomy
- 🟡 Multiple slugs (temple · museum · palace · monument · historical_site) map to `nex.brain_attractions.attraction_kind` CHECK enum

Interpretation: Cultural is Category-shaped inside Attractions Domain. Temples · museums · palaces · historic sites ARE attractions. Fits §7.4 C1 activity-taxonomy applicable_domains pattern (cultural activity-slugs → attractions Domain).

Master AI recommendation: **CATEGORY within Attractions Domain** (existing).

**22.2.4 · History · CATEGORY within Attractions + cross-Domain ACTIVITY**

Evidence:
- ❌ Not AgentId · not classifier · not brain vertical · no specialist substrate · no adapter · not Lab
- 🟡 `nex.brain_did_you_know_indonesia` 39 rows (Indonesian historical trivia)
- 🟡 Supabase `"NEX Trade Knowledge · Historical Context"` 9 rows (§7.1 inventory)
- 🟡 `nex_lab.growth_history` — operational Lab state history · NOT subject-matter history
- 🟡 CULTURE group slugs include `historic_site` · `heritage_area` — attractions-shape

Interpretation: History has two distinct usages: (a) historical-attraction PLACES (historic sites · heritage areas · palaces · monuments) — these are Attractions Categories · (b) historical-context ATTRIBUTES on entities (year-built · cultural-period · historical-significance) — cross-Domain activity/attribute per §7.6.5 G4 pattern.

Master AI recommendation: **CATEGORY within Attractions Domain** (for historical places) + **cross-Domain ACTIVITY / ATTRIBUTE** (for historical-context on entities in any Domain).

**22.2.5 · Places · ALIAS of Attractions Domain (existing §7.3)**

Evidence:
- ✅ **`places` IS a brain WorldVertical peer** (`frame-scope-intelligence.ts:350` · `["accommodation","food","commerce","transport","service","places"]`)
- ✅ Handled in composer (`compare-from-world.ts:258` case "places" · `orchestrate.ts:781` case "places" · `orchestrate.ts:2512` singular/plural label mapping)
- ✅ Presentation layer (`presentation.ts:418` singular="place" plural="places")
- ✅ **`recommendation-intent.ts:123 · attraction: "places", attractions: "places"`** — **attractions is EXPLICITLY MAPPED to places at recommendation-intent tier**
- ✅ `recommendation-intent.ts:120 · place: "places", places: "places", spot: "places", spots: "places"` — place synonyms
- ❌ No dedicated `places-postgres.ts` adapter
- ❌ Not AgentId · not classifier target (classifier has `attractions` · not `places`)
- 🟡 `nex.geo_landmark` 34 rows · `nex.brain_location` 0 rows · `nex.brain_local_guide` 0 rows

Interpretation: **`places` and `attractions` are architecturally coupled at multiple tiers**:
- Domain-classifier surface uses `attractions` (per §7.3 lock)
- Brain-vertical layer uses `places`
- recommendation-intent.ts:123 explicitly maps `attractions → places`
- brain_attractions specialist substrate exists (32 rows · per §7.3)

This is a §7.6-style alias question that emerged post-§7.6 lock. `places` is the brain-vertical form; `attractions` is the classifier form. They refer to the same subject-matter Domain (locations to visit · things to see).

Master AI recommendation: **ALIAS of Attractions Domain (canonical: attractions)** following §7.6.1 markets↔commerce pattern. Naming reconciliation authored in future implementation ADR (candidate slot ADR-0314a.2.ab · Places-Attractions Naming Reconciliation). Founder may amend the canonical direction (attractions vs places) at implementation time.

**22.2.6 · Locations · CROSS-DOMAIN METADATA (not a Domain)**

Evidence:
- ❌ Not AgentId · not classifier · not brain-vertical · no specialist substrate · no adapter · not Lab
- 🟡 `nex.brain_location` 0 rows (schema-ready · never populated)
- 🟡 In `recommendation-intent.ts:64` as part of place-synonym cluster (`place · places · location · locations`)
- ✅ EVERY existing Domain entity carries location data: `nex.accommodation_business` (city · district · coordinates_lat · coordinates_lng) · `nex.food_business` (same) · `nex.service_business` (same) · `nex.transport_acquisition_record` (same) · `nex.mp_seller` (city · jurisdiction) · every brain_attractions row (location_slug · coordinates_lat · coordinates_lng)

Interpretation: Location is a **cross-Domain metadata field** carried by every entity across all Domains. It is NOT itself a subject-matter Domain. This aligns with the founder's country-expansion note (2026-09-11): *"country/local context should be another governed dimension of the knowledge rather than another NEX brain."*

Master AI recommendation: **CROSS-DOMAIN METADATA** — Location is a governed classification dimension (city · district · country · coordinates · jurisdiction) applicable to entities across ALL Domains. Not a Domain. Not a Category. A separate architectural dimension.

Note for founder: this may be worth formalising as a **sixth architectural axis** (Location/Geography) alongside the five locked in §7.3 B1 — supporting country expansion (UK · USA · Australia · Ireland · Europe) without per-country Brains. But that's a future ADR · not §7.10 scope.

- ✅ `nex.sparks_product` 3 · `nex.sparks_product_price` 6 — adjacent commerce
- ✅ Commerce Domain locked per §7.5 D1 · products are commerce entities

Interpretation: Products are commerce entities. Fully covered by Commerce Domain. Not a separate Domain.

Master AI recommendation: **EXISTING DOMAIN · Commerce (§7.5 D1)**. Product-shape entities live in Commerce.

Master AI recommendation: **Operational Marketing = OPERATIONAL SCOPE (§7.3 B1 preserved)** · **subject-matter Marketing Domain = DEFER** — no new evidence since §7.3. Future ADR may promote if evidence emerges.

**22.2.10 · Trade Knowledge (staircase · door · kitchen · flooring · timber · etc.) · DEFER**

- ❌ Not classifier · not Lab · not NexVertical

Interpretation: The 11 slugs (staircase · door · interior · kitchen · bathroom · tools · timber · flooring · lighting · roofing · marketing) are ADR-0033 IMAGE-BRAIN isolation scopes · not Intelligence Domain candidates on their own. Under §7.5 D1 substrate-confluence test · each slug has 1 of 6 confluences (Supabase legacy category values only). Insufficient for individual Domain promotion.

Options for founder:

- (b) **Categories within Accommodation Domain** — some (kitchen · bathroom · interior · flooring) fit accommodation-adjacent · but door · timber · tools · staircase · roofing · lighting less so
- (c) **DEFER** pending ADR-0314a.2.aa Supabase Migration decision first · clean substrate before Domain classification

**Founder-provided constitutional interpretation (verbatim):**

> *"When a crawler finds a completely new business, NEX doesn't need to build a new brain. It tries: Business → Domain → Category → Entity → Activity/Offering → Location. If the category already exists, use it. If something genuinely doesn't fit, NEX identifies a taxonomy gap rather than blindly inventing a brain. That's the scalable system."*

> *"Locations are being recognised as information about everything, not another brain. That is exactly what we need for: Indonesia → UK → USA → Australia → Ireland → Europe → etc. The same business/service/product concepts can exist everywhere; country, city, coordinates and local context tell NEX where they exist."*

**Six locked constitutional rules across the layer:**

1. **R-DOMAIN-01** · three-axis anti-substitution (LAM Object Type · Domain · Category)
2. **§7.3 B1** · five-axis architecture (adds Operational Scope · Agent/Runtime Identity)
3. **§7.6.5 G4** · **Activity ≠ Domain** (activities are cross-Domain classifications applied to assets whose Domains remain authoritative)
4. **§7.7 H1** · **`other` ≠ `unknown`** · axis-specific (other = classified into governed catch-all · unknown = unresolved · silent conversion forbidden)
5. **§7.8 J1** · **Physical representation ≠ logical authority** (Rule 15 applied to Supabase category axis · field-specific NEX prefix treatment)
6. **§7.9 L1** · **Business ≠ Services** · sub-services are Categories inside Services · no per-service Brain inflation
7. **§7.10 M1** · **Domain axis is closed at 9** at Intelligence Domain tier · Categories/aliases/metadata/activities live INSIDE existing Domains · Location is candidate 6th architectural axis (future ADR)

**Anti-inflation discipline · 3 activity/cross-cutting classifications:**

- **Activity** (rental · booking · subscription · delivery · service-request · installation · repair · maintenance · hire · resale · fitting · cleaning · consultation · historical-context) — cross-Domain classification applied to assets in their own Domains (§7.6.5 G4)
- **Alias** (markets → commerce · places → attractions · food-beverage → food) — historical/legacy classifier value trending toward canonical Domain (§7.6.1 · §7.2 A1 · §7.10 M1)
- **Cross-Domain metadata** (Location · country · geography) — governed dimension applicable to entities across all Domains · candidate 6th architectural axis (§7.10 M1)

**Enforcement implications (doctrine · not implemented by this ADR):**

- Guardian rule: only the 9 canonical Domain slugs are permitted at Domain axis · use of any other value (Healthcare · Engineering · Cultural · History · Places · Products · Hotels · Trade Knowledge · Marketing subject-matter · Locations) as a Domain-typed value is a G-2 non-bypass violation until a future ADR promotes it
- Guardian rule: Category tier authoring per Domain proceeds via founder-authorised Category taxonomy ADRs (e.g. ADR-0314a.2.o for R-02 audience mapping · ADR-0314a.2.k for R-12 classification enum · Services Category Taxonomy · Attractions Category Taxonomy · etc.)
- Anti-inflation rule: Domain axis addition requires a founder-authorised ADR proving 5-of-6 substrate confluences (brain WorldVertical peer · dedicated adapter · specialist substrate · composer wiring · provider/companion ecosystem · classifier/AgentId/Lab/NexVertical membership)
- No physical row modified · no CHECK constraint added · no code change · no substrate mutation · no capabilities activated by §7.10 lock
